### [1] [Spillover Effects under Network Interference When Neighbours' Treatment Effects Are Heterogeneous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29882)
*Faezeh Dehghan Tarzjani,Bhaskar Krishnamachari*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出SpilloverNet，一种保留邻居级响应动态的图神经网络，用于优化预算约束下的网络干预传播预测。作者证明仅独立汇总邻居处理与属性的模型无法捕捉邻居对自身处理的响应强度这一关键交互信息，并指出从协变量估计邻居响应的plug-in方法存在不可约的误差下界（由未观测的个人响应性决定）。通过小规模直接响应测量获取单位级估计可突破该界限，在预算定位问题中恢复高达14个百分点的oracle最优福利。在两个真实社交图上，SpilloverNet达到7.7-8.4%的误差，优于标准GNN和专用因果表征基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型预测网络溢出效应时仅考虑邻居的处理状态和属性，忽略了每个邻居对自身处理的实际响应强度这一关键信息。在预算约束下的网络干预优化中，不仅需要知道谁被连接，还需预测个体接受者传播处理益处的强度，而缺乏对邻居响应动态的建模导致预测不准确，进而影响资源分配的效率。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明独立汇总邻居处理和属性的模型无法捕捉邻居响应与其处理的交互作用。随后提出SpilloverNet——一种保留邻居级响应动态的图神经网络架构。针对邻居响应不可观测的问题，作者分析了从协变量估计并plug-in的自然方法，并证明该方案存在由未观测个人响应性决定的不可约误差下界。最终方案是在溢出到达前通过小型试点实验进行直接响应测量，获得单位级估计以规避该误差界限。

Result: plug-in方法的误差在异质性增长时升至51.9%，比完全不使用响应估计还要差，但其总体相关性仍看似可接受。利用试点直接测量获得的单位级估计可突破不可约误差界限，在预算定位问题中恢复高达14个百分点的oracle最优福利。在两个真实社交图上，SpilloverNet达到7.7-8.4%的误差，优于标准GNN和专用因果表征基线。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了网络溢出效应预测中邻居响应动态的关键作用，建立了不可约误差的理论界限，并提供了实用的解决办法（试点直接测量）。SpilloverNet在实证中表现出显著优势，为预算约束下的网络干预优化提供了更为精准的工具。

Abstract: Optimizing budget-constrained network interventions requires evaluating not just who is connected, but predicting how strongly individual recipients will propagate the treatment's benefits. Existing models predict spillover from neighbors' treatments and attributes. We argue that spillover also depends on how strongly each neighbor responded to its own treatment. We prove that models summarizing neighbor treatments and attributes independently cannot capture this interaction, and we introduce SpilloverNet, a graph neural network designed to preserve neighbor-level response dynamics. Because a neighbor's response is unobserved, a natural approach is to estimate it from covariates and plug it in. However, we prove that any predictor relying solely on standard network data faces an irreducible error floor set by unobserved personal responsiveness. Empirically, the plug-in's error climbs to 51.9% as heterogeneity grows - worse than using no responsiveness estimate at all - while its overall correlation still looks acceptable. A per-unit estimate from a direct-response measurement, collected in a small pilot before spillover arrives, escapes this bound and recovers up to 14 percentage points of oracle-optimal welfare in a budgeted targeting problem. On two real social graphs, SpilloverNet reaches 7.7-8.4% error, outperforming standard GNNs as well as specialized causal-representation baselines.

</details>


### [2] [The PUR-1 Cyber-Physical Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30186)
*Vasileios Theos,Jonah Lau,Konstantinos Gkouliaras,Zachery Dahm,Konstantinos Vasili,Noah Fillgrove,William Richards,True Miller,Brian Jowers,Stylianos Chatzidakis*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了普渡大学反应堆一号数字孪生（PUR-1 DT），一个集成高保真物理模型与AI驱动的虚拟模型栈（中子学、热工水力、点动力学）的赛博物理数字孪生系统，通过双向通信与赛博物理测试平台实现对反应堆的闭环可解释诊断、预测、预测控制和行动推荐，并在完整反应堆运行周期上验证了实时同步状态估计和短期预测的准确性与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生技术有望提升核系统的运行灵活性和响应能力，但高效的数字孪生需要集成多种数据驱动和基于物理的模型，具备可解释性，同时以小于运行周期的时间常数保持与物理设施的双向同步。现有研究在完整的多模型集成与实时双向同步方面尚存在挑战。

Method: 构建PUR-1 DT赛博物理数字孪生系统，包含高保真的基于物理的虚拟模型栈（中子学、热工水力、点动力学）与AI驱动模型，通过双向通信和赛博物理测试平台实现闭环可解释诊断、预测、预测控制及行动推荐。在完整反应堆运行周期上进行了实时同步状态估计和短期预测的基准实验以验证准确性和延迟。

Result: 实验结果表明系统与实验结果吻合良好，实时同步状态估计和短期预测在完整运行周期上表现出良好的准确性，同时验证了延迟满足要求，为数字孪生功能在实际设施中的进一步开发与实验演示奠定了基础。

Conclusion: PUR-1 DT展示了将物理模型与AI模型集成的数字孪生方案在核反应堆运行支持中的可行性，验证了实时状态估计与预测的能力，为未来在实际设施中进一步开展数字孪生功能演示和应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Digital twin technologies have the potential to improve operational flexibility and responsiveness capabilities of nuclear systems. To provide decision support, cyber event characterization, state estimation, predictive control, and real-time dynamic processing of operational data, however, an efficient digital twin needs to integrate multiple models (data-driven as well as physics-based) with explainability while at the same time maintain two-way synchronization with the physical facility at a time constant less than its operational cycle. In this work, we present the Purdue University Reactor One Digital Twin (PUR-1 DT), a cyber-physical digital twin with a complete high-fidelity physics-based and AI-driven virtual model stack (neutronics, thermal-hydraulics, point kinetics) which provides closed-loop explainable diagnostics, forecasting, predictive control, and action recommendation back to the reactor via two-way communications and a cyber-physical testbed. We demonstrate real-time synchronized state estimation and short-term forecasting over a full reactor operational cycle and conduct a series of benchmarking experiments to validate accuracy and latency. Our results show good agreement with experimental results and lay the groundwork for further development and experimental demonstration of DT-enabled functionalities in real-world facilities.

</details>


### [3] [Domain-Grounded Tool Orchestration for LLM-Guided Scientific Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30696)
*Jeff Lee,Sebastien Jourdain,Cory Quammen,Patrick O'Leary,Berk Geveci*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将LLM的意图理解与确定性领域工具分离的架构，通过Model Context Protocol (MCP)连接，并由领域本体约束规划过程，确保科学分析工作流（如CFD后处理和拓扑数据分析）的执行正确性。该架构在ParaView服务器上实例化，覆盖两个领域仅需添加本体和工具包装器。消融实验表明本体的作用不在于改变工具选择，而在于纠正模型对结果的理解，将解释准确率从0.41提升到0.91（关键前提是相关信息以作用域而非批量形式检索）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM辅助科学可视化方法直接生成脚本，将领域知识隐式编码在代码中，常常产生能执行但结果错误的脚本。科学分析工作流（如CFD工程师调查流动分离时需要依次提取壁面剪切应力、识别摩擦系数的零交叉、并用边界层剖面确认）依赖于紧密耦合的操作序列，其正确性取决于工具选择、执行顺序和参数化，而LLM直接编写代码的方法无法可靠保证这种正确性。

Method: 提出三阶段分离架构：(1) 意图解释由LLM完成，(2) 执行由确定性领域工具完成，(3) 解释说明由LLM完成，三个组件通过Model Context Protocol (MCP)连接，并由领域本体约束规划过程，将策略限制在合法的分析链中。架构在ParaView服务器基础设施上实例化到两个领域：计算流体动力学后处理和基于Topology ToolKit (TTK)的拓扑数据分析。新增领域仅需提供本体和现有过滤器的工具包装器。消融实验分离了本体的效应。

Result: 架构设计从根本上消除了脚本生成中的整类失败模式（如API幻觉和流水线阶段缺失），并缩小了剩余策略错误的范围。在两个领域的消融实验表明：本体并不改变规划器选择的工具（工具选择本身已可靠），而是纠正模型对结果的解释方式，将解释准确率从0.41提升至0.91；且此效果仅在相关事实以作用域（scoped）而非批量（bulk）形式检索时出现。新增第二个领域无需改变架构、协议或部署。

Conclusion: 将意图理解、确定性执行和结果解释解耦的架构，配合领域本体约束和MCP协议连接，是提升LLM辅助科学可视化可靠性的有效路径。该方法通过架构性设计消除特定失败类别，通过本体增强结果解释准确率，并展现了良好的可扩展性（新领域仅需本体和工具包装器）。

Abstract: Scientific analysis workflows encode deep domain knowledge through sequences of tightly coupled operations where correctness depends on tool selection, execution order, and parameterization. A CFD engineer investigating flow separation must extract wall shear stress, identify zero-crossings in skin friction, and confirm with boundary-layer profiles: a chain that requires both domain expertise and proficiency with visualization tools. Current approaches to LLM-assisted scientific visualization generate scripts that encode this knowledge implicitly, and often incorrectly, producing code that executes but yields wrong results. We present an architecture that separates intent interpretation (LLM) from execution (deterministic domain tools) from explanation (LLM), connected by the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and grounded by domain ontologies that constrain planning to valid analysis chains. We instantiate the architecture in two domains on the same ParaView server infrastructure: computational fluid dynamics post-processing and topological data analysis via the Topology ToolKit (TTK). Adding the second domain required only an ontology and tool wrappers around existing filters, with no change to the architecture, protocol, or deployment. By construction the design removes whole classes of failure that affect script generation (such as API hallucination and missing pipeline stages) and narrows the strategic errors that remain. An ablation across both domains locates the ontology's empirical effect: it does not change which tools the planner selects, which is already reliable, but corrects how the model interprets results, raising interpretation accuracy from 0.41 to 0.91, and only when the relevant fact is retrieved in scoped rather than bulk form. ParaView's client-server model carries analysis to production-scale datasets through a thin browser client.

</details>

### [4] [libNLPBE: An Open-Source Python Package for Solving the Non-Linear Poisson-Boltzmann Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28908)
*Jun-Hyeong Kim,Weitao Yang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了libNLPBE，一个开源的Python库，用于在分子电子结构计算中求解非线性泊松-玻尔兹曼方程（NLPBE），以高效处理电解质溶液环境对溶质化学性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电解质溶液中移动离子通过静电相互作用显著影响溶质的化学性质，但现有的NLPBE求解器在分子电子结构计算中可用性有限，阻碍了对离子效应的理论研究。

Method: 该库采用密度拟合（DF）近似高效计算溶质静电势，并开发了修改的Damped Inexact Newton Multigrid（mDINMH）方法求解NLPBE，包括使用对称预条件子求解牛顿方程、集成代数多重网格方法以支持广泛的网格点，以及提供GPU加速版本。

Result: libNLPBE提供了与DFT计算相结合的NLPBE求解能力，可获取电解质溶液环境对Fock矩阵的静电修正项，并支持GPU并行化以提高计算效率。

Conclusion: libNLPBE作为开源工具提升了NLPBE在分子电子结构计算中的可及性，为在电解质溶液环境中进行化学设计的理论研究提供了高效平台。

Abstract: Solvent environments surrounding a solute can significantly alter its chemical properties. These changes become more prominent in electrolyte solutions, where mobile ions largely influence the solute through electrostatic interactions. A theoretical description of these ionic effects will benefit the design of chemistry performed in electrolyte solutions. As a result, the non-linear Poisson-Boltzmann equation (NLPBE) has emerged as an efficient implicit solvent model for electronic structure calculations to address such effects. However, the limited availability of NLPBE solvers for molecular electronic structure calculations greatly hinders theoretical investigation into the ionic effects. To improve accessibility of the NLPBE, we present libNLPBE, an open-source Python library, that solves the NLPBE for molecular systems in combination with density functional calculations, specifically to obtain electrostatic correction terms arising from the electrolyte solution environment for the Fock matrix. The library employs the density fitting (DF) approximation to efficiently calculate solute electrostatic potentials. Furthermore, we develop the modified Damped Inexact Newton Multigrid developed by Holst (mDINMH) method for solving the NLPBE. The mDINMH features a symmetric preconditioner for solving the Newton equation, which enables the use of robust multigrid methods designed for symmetric linear operators. In addition, an algebraic multigrid method has been incorporated into the mDINMH to support a broad range of grid points. We also present a GPU-accelerated version of libNLPBE to leverage parallelization efficiency of GPUs.

</details>
