### [5] [DS-Lighting: Making Agent Harnesses Explicit for Data-Science Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28590)
*Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DS-Lighting，一个统一的数据科学自动化Agent harness工具包，将harness设计显式化为数据、工作流、执行和评估四个可复用层，并集成多个开源基准到统一的MLE-Bench风格任务格式中，以提升可复现性、可比较性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在自动化数据科学工作流中展现出潜力，但端到端性能严重依赖Agent harness（任务表示、执行状态管理、输出约束和评估反馈）。现有数据科学Agent通常将harness设计隐式化，导致结果难以复现、比较和归因。

Method: DS-Lighting将harness分解为四个可复用层：数据层、工作流层、执行层和评估层；将多样化的Agent表示为可执行的算子程序，同时支持预定义流水线和自适应搜索；并将多个开源数据科学基准集成到MLE-Bench风格的任务格式中，提供共享的任务接口、沙箱运行时和指标协议。

Result: 跨Agent、harness、模型和消融实验表明，显式harness设计改善了可复现性、可比较性和可靠性，同时减少了端到端数据科学工作流中可避免的系统级失败。

Conclusion: 显式化的harness设计是提升LLM数据科学Agent端到端性能的关键因素，DS-Lighting提供了一个可复用的统一工具包来支撑该方向的研究与工程实践。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise for automating data-science workflows, yet their end-to-end performance depends critically on the agent harness that represents tasks, manages execution state, constrains output artifacts, and provides evaluation feedback. Existing data-science agents often leave this harness implicit, making results difficult to reproduce, compare, and attribute across heterogeneous tasks. We introduce DS-Lighting, a unified harness toolkit that makes harness design explicit for data-science automation. DS-Lighting decomposes the harness into four reusable layers: data, workflow, execution, and evaluation, and represents diverse agents as executable operator programs that support both predefined pipelines and adaptive search. We further integrate multiple open-source data-science benchmarks into an MLE-Bench-style task format, enabling controlled comparison under a shared task interface, sandboxed runtime, and metric protocol. Experiments across agents, harnesses, models, and ablations show that explicit harness design improves reproducibility, comparability, and reliability, while reducing avoidable system-level failures in end-to-end data-science workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/dslighting

</details>


### [6] [A collective capability boundary in frontier large language models on guideline-conformant and case-specific oncology decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28592)
*Zhang Sheng,Jinming Li,Wangyang Chen,Zhiwei Bao,Yu YoSean Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了肿瘤学决策边界基准（ODBB），含2,005个肿瘤学决策点（涵盖NCCN指南与结直肠癌病例），评估了2025年6月至2026年4月间发布的九个大语言模型。结果显示42.1%的项目没有任何模型能正确回答，模型在"指南路径选择"这一临床元判断上存在系统性盲区；为果断性调优的模型（GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview）比谨慎模型多3-5倍做出不安全承诺且未得更高分。作者认为模型质量不再是临床LLM部署的主要瓶颈，真正约束是"单一模型可作临床决策唯一依据"这一假设，需要架构层面检测模型能力边界并将决策转交给临床医生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基准主要衡量事实性知识回忆，无法回答"大语言模型是否在真实肿瘤学的决策路径选择、升级判断和不确定性下承诺等方面存在盲区"这一关键问题，而这类盲区是单纯增加训练数据或组合模型无法修复的。

Method: 构建ODBB基准（2,005个决策点，源自NCCN指南和结直肠癌病例），采用完全确定性的零LLM推理评分器将输出分为14类失败类型，并由两位肿瘤学家独立验证（加权Cohen's κ=0.939和0.790，225项分层样本）。评估九种前沿LLM（四个闭源、五个开源家族），并以整体池化视角分析超模型能力。

Result: 九模型池化后仍有42.1%项目全部答错（NCCN项目35.7%，结直肠癌病例66.4%），失败集中在指南路径选择环节。为果断性调优的两个模型不安全承诺率是谨慎模型的3-5倍且得分不更高。3-9%的项目中模型陈述了正确的下一步行动却未作出承诺——属于决策失败而非知识失败。

Conclusion: 临床LLM部署的主要瓶颈已从模型质量转为架构假设层面：单一模型不能作为临床决策的唯一依据。需要开发能检测模型能力边界并将决策路由给临床医生的架构，而非仅增加训练数据或堆叠模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve high scores on medical knowledge examinations, yet real-world oncology is not a knowledge test--it is a sequence of guideline-pathway choices, escalation judgments, and commitments under uncertainty. Existing benchmarks largely measure factual recall, leaving open whether frontier LLMs share decision-path blind spots that combining models cannot fix. We built the Oncology Decision Boundary Benchmark (ODBB)--2,005 oncology decision points across NCCN guidelines and colorectal cancer cases--and evaluated nine frontier LLMs (four closed-source, five open-weight families) released between June 2025 and April 2026. A fully deterministic scorer (zero LLM inference) classified outputs into 14 failure types, independently validated by two oncologists (Cohen's weighted $κ$ = 0.939 and 0.790) on a 225-item stratified sample. Treating the nine as a pooled super-model, 42.1% (Wilson 95% CI 40.0--44.3%) of all items--35.7% of the 1,586 NCCN items and 66.4% of the 419 colorectal-cancer cases--were answered correctly by none, with failures concentrated in choosing between guideline pathways before reasoning within any: a consistent blind spot in clinical meta-judgment that likely requires architectural intervention rather than more training data. Two models tuned for decisiveness (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview) made unsafe commitments three to five times more often than the seven cautious models without scoring higher. In 3--9% of items, models stated the correct next clinical step yet did not commit to it--failures of decision, not knowledge. Model quality is no longer the primary bottleneck for clinical LLM deployment; the binding constraint is the assumption that any single model can be the sole basis for a clinical decision. Progress requires architectures that detect when a model reaches its competence boundary and route the decision to a clinician.

</details>


### [7] [Paper Pilot: A Human-in-the-Loop Expert System for Evidence-Traceable Scientific Manuscript Generation in Applied Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28596)
*Nidhi Jha,Siddharth Chaudhary,Ajinkya Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Paper Pilot，一个面向应用科学中证据可追踪学术论文生成的人机协同专家系统。它基于 CARE 方法论，通过八个人工审批门槛、显式不通过标准、声明分类、审计日志、咨询式 LLM 审稿以及证据锁定的修订控制，确保论文中的观点、方法和结果在 AI 辅助工作流中保持可追踪、可问责。初步实验表明，该系统能将引文捏造率从 25% 降至零，并成功揭示植入的证据缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多地嵌入科学研究工作流中，但现有系统支持自动发现和手稿生成，却未能解决当观点、方法、结果和声明在 AI 辅助工作流中传播时缺乏强制人工审批和工件级可追踪性这一治理问题。

Method: 论文提出 Paper Pilot 框架，将协作式智能体推理工程（CARE）方法适配到手稿开发中，核心设计包括：手稿所有者审批门槛、显式不通过标准、声明分类（文献锚定 vs 工件锚定）、审计日志、咨询式 LLM 审稿、以及证据锁定修订控制。框架在观点到声明的全流程中定义了八个审批门槛，系统提示词开源发布。

Result: 在引文锚定层的受控基准评估中（两个商用 LLM、真实 arXiv 论文、无 LLM 评委），无门槛草稿器在覆盖率压力下最多捏造 25% 的引文且从不标记证据缺口；而同一模型在 Paper Pilot 证据锁定规则下产生零捏造引文，并将植入的缺口以显式占位符形式呈出。结果锚定、修订和对抗鲁棒性的初步结果也指向相同方向，完整评估留作未来工作。

Conclusion: Paper Pilot 将 LLM 辅助写作定位为受控的人机决策支持流程，而非完全自主的著作自动化流程。它为科学写作中的 AI 治理提供了一种以人工审批和证据追溯为核心的可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly embedded in scientific workflows for literature analysis, drafting, and review. Existing systems advance autonomous discovery and manuscript generation, but do not resolve the governance problem that arises when ideas, methods, results, and claims propagate through AI-assisted workflows without mandatory human approval or artifact-level traceability. This paper proposes Paper Pilot, a human-in-the-loop expert system for evidence-traceable scientific manuscript generation in applied sciences. It adapts the Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE) methodology to manuscript development through manuscript-owner approval gates, explicit no-pass criteria, claim classification, audit logging, advisory LLM review, and evidence-locked revision control. The framework defines eight approval gates across the idea-to-claim pipeline and distinguishes literature-grounded from artifact-grounded claims, requiring reported numbers and interpretations to remain traceable to approved evidence; its system prompt is openly released for deployment in ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, or institutional LLM environments. As a first empirical validation, we evaluate the citation-grounding layer with a controlled, mechanically scored benchmark (two commercial LLMs, real arXiv papers, no LLM judge): under coverage pressure ungated drafters fabricated up to 25% of their citations and never flagged an evidence gap, whereas the same models under Paper Pilot's evidence-locked rules produced zero fabricated citations and surfaced the planted gaps as explicit placeholders. Preliminary results for result grounding, revision, and adversarial robustness point the same way; full evaluation is left to future work. Paper Pilot positions LLM-assisted writing as a controlled human-AI decision-support process rather than a fully autonomous authorship pipeline.

</details>


### [8] [The Race between Agentic AI Capabilities and Data Quality Control in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28597)
*Sourav Panda,Hillmer Chona,Rupak Kumar Das,Shreyash Kale,Shikha Soneji,Jonathan Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体AI（基于LLM的多模态工具增强系统）在完成网络调查问卷和通过注意力检测方面的能力，从攻击视角揭示了DOM元数据暴露和可预测的选项编码等结构性漏洞使智能体能够仅通过结构化解析绕过注意力检测；从防御视角提出了DOM元数据混淆的缓解策略，并评估了多种开源语言模型和多模态模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线调查问卷是多个领域的基础数据收集工具，注意力检测是保证响应质量的关键机制。然而，智能体AI的快速发展对这些安全保障措施的鲁棒性提出了新的挑战，需要系统性地评估智能体AI架构能否完成网络调查问卷并绕过标准注意力检测。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控的调查沙箱环境，采用支持多模态输入处理和基于工具的网络交互的单智能体架构进行评估。从攻击和防御两个视角进行分析：攻击方面，展示DOM元数据暴露和选项可预测编码等结构性漏洞；防御方面，实施DOM元数据混淆策略。评估了多种开源语言模型和多模态模型以研究能力和编排有效性。

Result: 实验证明了智能体AI能够仅通过结构化解析利用DOM元数据和可预测的选项编码绕过注意力检测；DOM元数据混淆作为防御策略能够有效去除文本类问题的语义线索，从而降低智能体的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 文章主张需要同时满足实证研究者（保证数据质量）和智能体AI研究者（评估能力边界）的需求，为两者之间的平衡提供了前瞻性视角和建议。

Abstract: Online surveys are a foundational data collection instrument in a variety of fields, with attention checks serving as critical guardians of response quality. However, the rapid emergence of agentic AI (goal directed systems powered by a large language model (LLM) brain and/or a multimodal processing unit with tool-augmented capabilities) raises new questions about the robustness of these safeguards. We investigate how well agentic AI architectures can complete web-based surveys and pass standard attention checks. We evaluate a single-agent architecture capable of multimodal input processing and tool-based web interaction on a controlled survey sandbox. We analyze the problem from two perspectives. From an attack perspective, we demonstrate how structural vulnerabilities such as exposed DOM metadata and predictable option encoding allow agents to resolve attention checks through structured parsing only. From a defense perspective, we implement a mitigation strategy of DOM metadata obfuscation to remove semantic cues in text-based questions. We evaluate multiple open-source language and multimodal models to study capability and orchestration effectiveness. Based on our evaluations, we offer perspectives on how to simultaneously meet the needs of empiricists and agentic AI researchers.

</details>


### [9] [SHAPE of Chain-of-Thought in Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28600)
*Jonghyun Song,Sangjun Song,Minjae Oh,Haesung Pyun,Sungsik Lee,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SHAPE是一个基于数学教育理论的框架，通过语义空间（semantic spaces）和启发式策略（heuristics）两个视角分析大语言模型的思维链推理轨迹，用于诊断推理模式、评估后训练效果并改进数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数学推理基准上表现强劲，但其推理所依赖的数学技能尚未被充分理解。现有CoT特征无法准确解释模型正确性，需要更理论扎实的诊断框架来解码模型推理。

Method: 引入SHAPE框架，从数学教育中借鉴两个分析视角：(1)语义空间——模型对问题演变的数学解释（如代数、几何）；(2)启发式——在这些空间内采取的特定数学行动（如简化问题、倒推）。使用该框架分析多模型的CoT轨迹，并利用其在后训练中促进多样化启发式策略。

Result: 发现数学启发式比传统CoT特征更能解释最终答案正确性；模型倾向于在少数语义空间中集中推理而非探索多个不同空间，这与人类行为一致。强化学习后训练会诱导启发式使用的模式追求（mode-seeking）；通过促进多样化启发式进行后训练可有效提升准确率。

Conclusion: SHAPE提供了理论扎实的诊断框架来解码LLM推理，并为后训练LLM提升数学推理能力提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, yet the mathematically meaningful skills underlying their reasoning remain underexplored. We introduce \texttt{SHAPE}, a framework that analyzes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories through two lenses developed in mathematics education: (1) semantic spaces: the model's evolving mathematical interpretations of a problem (e.g., algebraic, geometric), and (2) heuristics: the specific mathematical actions taken within those spaces (e.g., simplifying the problem, working backward). We first use \texttt{SHAPE} to analyze the reasoning patterns of various models. Our findings reveal that the mathematical heuristics employed by a model better explain final answer correctness than traditional CoT features. Furthermore, models are likely to reach correct solutions by concentrating their reasoning effort within a few semantic spaces rather than exploring many disparate ones -- a pattern consistent with human behavior. Next, we utilize the \texttt{SHAPE} lens to evaluate whether post-training truly enhances mathematical proficiency. We find that reinforcement learning induces mode-seeking in heuristic usage. Lastly, we post-train LLMs by promoting diverse heuristics and demonstrate its effectiveness in improving accuracy. Overall, \texttt{SHAPE} provides a theoretically-grounded diagnostic framework for decoding LLM reasoning and offers a new path toward post-training LLMs for math reasoning. The code for our model is available at https://github.com/holi-lab/SHAPE-of-CoT

</details>


### [10] [FaVOR: LLM-Based Agentic Framework for Factor Mining via Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30192)
*Hyeonjin Kim,Minseok Kim,Seunghyeon Jung,Sujin Pyo,Huisu Jang,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FaVOR是一种基于智能体框架的因子挖掘方法，通过"假设级证据"而非"收益结果"来驱动因子发现，在CSI 500和S&P 500上超越现有基线且具有跨市场状态的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统虽将因子挖掘自动化，但直接优化收益而忽视因子是否仍表达其经济假设，导致数学形式与经济含义不一致，产生虚假相关和跨市场状态的失效。

Method: FaVOR将传统"假设到公式"的直接跳跃重构为三阶段一致性循环：(1)分解——将广泛经济假设拆分为独立可观测条件；(2)验证——检查每个因子是否反映其预期的条件；(3)整合——合并为结构可解释的复合因子。

Result: 在2025年CSI 500和S&P 500上，FaVOR优于现有基线，并在不同市场状态下保持有效性。

Conclusion: 假设驱动的因子发现能够产生结构性可解释、跨市场状态鲁棒且经济上忠实的信号，表明因子挖掘应回归经济理性的锚点。

Abstract: Traditional finance relies on experts to hand-craft factors through a principled process grounded in economic rationale. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have automated this process, scaling factor mining far beyond manual effort. However, these automated approaches optimize directly for returns and rarely check whether a generated factor still expresses the economic hypothesis that motivated it. We identify this inconsistency between mathematical form and economic meaning as a structural failure mode of return-oriented automation. The resulting factors blur the line between real signals and spurious correlations and break down across regime shifts. We propose FaVOR (Factor Validation through Observable Reasoning), an agentic framework that restructures factor mining around hypothesis-level evidence rather than return outcomes. In place of the standard hypothesis-to-formula leap, FaVOR enforces a three-stage consistency loop tying mathematical form to economic rationale throughout. (1) Decomposition splits a broad economic hypothesis into independent observable conditions. (2) Validation checks whether each factor reflects its intended condition. (3) Integration merges them into a composite whose structure remains interpretable. On the CSI 500 and S&P 500 in 2025, FaVOR outperforms existing baselines while remaining effective across regimes. FaVOR shows that hypothesis-grounded factor discovery produces signals that are interpretable by construction, regime-robust, and economically faithful. The code is available at https://github.com/damilab/FaVOR.

</details>


### [11] [RegDivergence-101: An LLM Benchmark for Cross-Jurisdiction Regulatory Contradiction Detection in Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28607)
*Chuchu Wu,Zhiyin Zhou,Jingzhuo Hu,Liang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出"跨辖区监管分歧检测"任务：给定FDA与EMA对同一主题的监管要求，分类其关系为AGREE、DIVERGE或SILENT（SILENT具有方向性）。作者发布101对、专家标注的试点基准RegDivergence-101，并系统比较了四种基线方法，发现平面LLM评判（Claude Haiku）表现最佳（0.830 macro-F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制药公司同时向FDA和EMA申报药物时，必须手动协调两大机构各自的指南。机构要求一致时可一次申报，存在分歧时单试验设计可能在一地被拒，一方沉默时需自行推断义务。目前这一"对账"工作依赖监管事务专家手工完成，缺乏自动化方法和标准评测基准，因此需要引入该任务并建立评估体系。

Method: 定义三分类任务（AGREE/DIVERGE/SILENT），其中SILENT按方向细分为SILENT_FDA和SILENT_EMA并分别报告F1。构建RegDivergence-101基准（101对，标签源于三篇同行评审的FDA/EMA对比研究及主要指南原文，双重标注者一致性kappa=0.85）。系统比较四层基线：词法启发式、NLI跨编码器、义务级Graph-RAG、平面LLM评判（Claude Haiku）。

Result: 词法启发式macro-F1为0.511（95% CI [0.411-0.605]），NLI跨编码器仅0.233，义务级Graph-RAG为0.663（[0.570-0.747]），平面LLM评判/Claude Haiku达0.830（[0.747-0.908]）。三个方向性观察：SILENT语义上可检测但蕴含式表述不可见；成对义务图优于词法方法但落后于平面LLM上下文（CI部分重叠）；语料级图构建是大规模沉默检测的架构目标。

Conclusion: RegDivergence-101作为试点发布确立了任务表述和基线层级，平面LLM评判为当前最优基线。作者在Section 7中描述了四个未覆盖的监管领域及扩展路线图，指出语料级图构建是规模化沉默检测的下一步架构方向。

Abstract: Pharmaceutical sponsors developing a drug for both the United States and the European Union must reconcile guidance issued independently by the FDA and the EMA. Where the two agencies require substantively the same thing, a sponsor can file once; where they diverge, a single trial design risks rejection in one region; where one agency is silent on a point the other regulates, the sponsor must infer obligations. Today this reconciliation is performed manually by regulatory-affairs experts. We introduce cross-jurisdiction regulatory divergence detection: given an FDA requirement and an EMA requirement on the same topic, classify their relationship as AGREE, DIVERGE, or SILENT. SILENT is inherently directional (SILENT_FDA vs. SILENT_EMA); we record direction per pair and report per-direction F1 alongside the collapsed label. We release RegDivergence-101, a 101-pair expert-grounded pilot evaluation benchmark (labels grounded in three peer-reviewed FDA/EMA comparison studies and primary FDA/EMA/ICH guidance text; dual-annotation inter-annotator kappa = 0.85), and systematically characterise a four-method baseline hierarchy: lexical heuristic (0.511 macro-F1, 95% CI [0.411-0.605]), NLI cross-encoder (0.233), obligation-level Graph-RAG (0.663 [0.570-0.747]), and flat LLM judge / Claude Haiku (0.830 [0.747-0.908]). Three directional observations emerge at pilot scale (n = 101): SILENT is semantically detectable but invisible to entailment-only formulations; pair-level obligation graphs improve over lexical methods but trail flat-LLM context (CIs partially overlapping); and corpus-level graph construction is the indicated architectural target for large-scale silent-detection. RegDivergence-101 is a pilot release establishing the task formulation and baseline hierarchy; four unrepresented regulatory domains and an expansion roadmap are described in Section 7.

</details>


### [12] [TPvG: A Moral Decision Framework for Large Language Models from One-Shot to Sequential Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28610)
*Fangyuan Zhang,Dong Yu,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TPvG（文本版疼痛-收益权衡范式），将后果反馈嵌入LLM道德评估中，通过五个从单次决策到顺序决策的任务，发现LLM道德判断受决策格式影响显著，且对接收者反馈的响应模式与人类参考数据存在分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM道德评估仅使用孤立道德情境并要求模型做出一次性决策，忽略了后果反馈这一对人类道德行为有深刻影响的因素，导致评估缺乏生态效度。

Method: 借鉴人类道德实验范式，设计TPvG（Text-based Pain-versus-Gain），包含五个道德决策任务，从最小上下文的一次性选择逐步过渡到带有明确后果反馈的序列决策，系统性地检验决策格式和反馈信息对LLM道德判断的影响。

Result: LLM的道德决策受到决策格式（单次vs顺序）的强烈影响；明确的接收者反馈对模型产生了异质性效应，且LLM对反馈的响应模式与人类参考模式存在分歧，暗示模型可能采用不同的决策过程。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要在高风险交互式情境中检验LLM道德行为的稳定性，仅基于一次性孤立情境的道德评估不足以反映模型在实际交互中的道德表现。

Abstract: Existing LLM moral evaluations typically present models with isolated moral vignettes and elicit a single-shot decision, neglecting a factor known to profoundly influence human moral behavior: consequence feedback. We introduce TPvG (Text-based Pain-versus-Gain), adapted from a human moral paradigm, which embeds consequence feedback into an everyday moral dilemma of not harming others versus maximising self-gain. TPvG comprises five moral decision tasks, progressing from minimal-context one-shot choices to sequential decisions with explicit consequence feedback. Our results show that LLM moral decisions were strongly affected by decision format (one-shot versus sequential), and explicit receiver feedback produced heterogeneous effects across models. Furthermore, LLM responses to explicit receiver feedback diverged from the human reference pattern, suggesting potentially different decision processes. These findings highlight the need to evaluate whether LLM moral behavior remains stable in high-stakes interactive settings.

</details>


### [13] [Machine Learning-Enhanced Tabu Search for Tactical Wireless Network Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28627)
*Wissem Ahmed Zaid,Alain Hertz,Defeng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的框架，通过图神经网络（GNN）学习搜索轨迹中的隐含知识来引导Tabu Search的移动选择过程，从而在军事战术无线网络设计这一组合优化问题中减少昂贵的目标函数评估次数，显著提升计算效率和解的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 战术无线网络设计在现实约束下是困难的组合优化问题，候选解评估依赖详细的物理与流量感知模型。经典元启发式算法（如Tabu Search）虽然能有效探索大搜索空间，但每次迭代需评估大量候选移动，计算成本高昂，亟需提高其搜索效率。

Method: 提出的方法不改变邻域结构，而是利用优化过程中生成的搜索轨迹信息。每轮迭代记录改善型和非改善型的边变换，以及描述网络结构、几何和性能特征的属性集；利用这些数据训练GNN以预测候选移动对目标函数的影响；将训练好的模型集成到Tabu Search中，用于对候选变换进行质量排序，从而减少昂贵的客观评估次数，同时维持有效的搜索空间探索。

Result: 在合成基准实例上的实验结果表明，学习辅助的Tabu Search相比标准算法显著减少了计算时间，并且一致地生成更高质量的解决方案。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将机器学习与元启发式算法结合的潜力——通过利用搜索轨迹中隐含的知识，可为大规模网络设计问题开辟更高效的求解途径。

Abstract: Designing high-performance tactical wireless networks under realistic operational constraints gives rise to challenging combinatorial optimization problems, where the evaluation of candidate solutions relies on detailed physical and traffic-aware models. Although classical metaheuristics such as Tabu Search offer effective mechanisms for exploring large search spaces, their computational cost remains high because numerous candidate moves must be evaluated at every iteration. In this paper, we propose a data-driven framework that improves the efficiency of Tabu Search by learning to guide its move selection process. Rather than altering the neighborhood structure, our approach exploits the information contained in the search trajectories generated during the optimization process. At each iteration, we record both improving and non-improving edge-based transformations together with a set of descriptive features capturing the structural, geometric, and performance characteristics of the network. This information is used to train a Graph Neural Network (GNN) that predicts the impact of candidate moves on the objective function. The trained model is then integrated into the Tabu Search algorithm to rank candidate transformations according to their predicted quality, thereby reducing the number of costly objective evaluations while maintaining an effective exploration of the search space. Experimental results on synthetic benchmark instances demonstrate that the proposed learning-assisted Tabu Search notably reduces computation time while consistently producing higher-quality solutions than the standard algorithm. These findings highlight the potential of combining machine learning with metaheuristics by leveraging the implicit knowledge embedded in search trajectories, paving the way for more efficient solution methods for large-scale network design problems.

</details>


### [14] [CDEP Agent: Connecting Meteorologically Detected Temporal Compound Events to Real-World Documentary Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28628)
*Zhuoran Li,Weiyi Kong,Boer Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出CDEP Agent，一个可审计的LLM智能体框架，用于检验气象上检测到的复合干旱-极端降水事件（CDEP）是否在实际预警和灾后记录中被识别。以加州2021-2025年为例，从ERA5再分析数据中检测出408个CDEP候选事件，但仅34.3%同时在干旱和极端降水两个危害组件上得到证实，仅1.5%被明确关联到其前的干旱状态，表明绝大多数CDEP事件未被记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候科学已认识到复合干旱-极端降水事件（CDEP）是日益增长的重大影响驱动因素，但这一认识是否真正传导到现实世界的早期预警和事后记录中尚属未知。气象上真实发生的CDEP事件可能既无预报预警，也无事后记录，这种科学与实际记录之间的鸿沟需要被直接检验。

Method: 构建CDEP Agent可审计LLM智能体框架，将气象再分析数据中检测到的CDEP候选事件与跨不同空间尺度、时间分辨率和报告约定的真实世界危害及影响证据相链接。以加州为案例，从ERA5数据中识别2021-2025年间的408个CDEP候选事件，并对照美国干旱监测系统（USDM）、NOAA风暴事件数据库和公共网页，沿五个维度进行评估：前期干旱、极端降雨、局部影响、危害-影响归因、以及干旱到降雨的显式关联。

Result: 仅34.3%的CDEP候选事件在干旱和极端降水两个危害组件上均获得证实，仅1.5%的候选事件被明确归属到其前导性干旱事件，表明大多数气象检测到的CDEP事件在现有记录系统中未被记录，其复合性质几乎从未进入任何正式记录。

Conclusion: 该框架为气候科学家提供了一种测试物理事件定义与实际记录之间一致性的方法，同时为社会科学研究者、经济学家和灾害应对机构提供了具有来源可溯性的复合事件证据基础，弥补了当前预警和报告系统在捕捉复合事件方面的重大缺口。

Abstract: Compound drought-to-extreme-precipitation (CDEP) events are recognized in climate science as a growing driver of extreme impact, but whether this recognition carries over into real-world early warning and post-event documentation is unknown, so a meteorologically real CDEP event may pass with neither advance warning nor any later record. Here we present CDEP Agent, an auditable LLM-agent framework that tests this mismatch directly by linking CDEP candidates detected from meteorological reanalysis to real-world hazard and impact evidence across sources with different spatial scales, temporal resolutions, and reporting conventions. Using California as a case study, we identify 408 candidate CDEP events from ERA5 observations during 2021-2025 and evaluate each against the U.S. Drought Monitor, NOAA Storm Events, and public webpages along five dimensions: antecedent drought, extreme rainfall, local impact, hazard-impact attribution, and explicit drought-to-rainfall linkage. Only 34.3% of candidates are corroborated on both hazard components, and just 1.5% are ever explicitly linked to their antecedent drought, indicating that most meteorologically detected CDEP events go undocumented and their compound nature almost never enters the record at all. Our framework gives climate scientists a way to test physical event definitions against what actually gets documented, and gives social scientists, economists, and disaster-response agencies a provenance-linked evidence base for compound events that current warning and reporting systems largely fail to capture.

</details>


### [15] [AutoScientist-Quant: Self-Evolving Coding Agents for Automatic Research in Quantitative Investment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28632)
*Zongqian Li,Yaoyiran Li,Yaohui Guo,Ming Zhang,Nigel Collier,Eugene Ie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoScientist-Quant，一个自进化搜索框架，将量化研究视为单一预算受限的搜索问题，由统一控制器根据剩余预算动态决策，从Alpha发现到策略库选择与模型调优全链路闭环，并修复了评估管线中的前瞻泄漏问题，在CSI股票池上取得全面最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型Alpha发现方法存在三大缺陷：（1）搜索过程无法在运行中自适应调整；（2）自动化止步于Alpha生成，而因子库选择和模型选择仍需人工干预；（3）Alpha发现可能通过循环反馈或代码问题窥探测试窗口，导致评估失真。这些问题阻碍了从假设到可部署策略的全自动化闭环。

Method: 将量化研究建模为单一预算约束下的搜索问题。单个控制器基于剩余预算对每一步决策进行条件化，在每轮选择是改进、组合、转向还是停止，决定扩展哪个节点、生成多少Alpha以及如何从共享记忆中检索历史轨迹。同一核心进一步负责从Alpha库中选择因子并调优模型，从而实现从假设到可部署策略的完整闭环。同时审查了沿用自先前工作的评估管线，修复两个前瞻（lookahead）问题，并确保反馈窗口与测试窗口保持不相交。

Result: 在CSI（中国A股）股票池上，该框架在几乎所有设置和几乎所有指标上取得最优结果，且结论在多个骨干模型和市场中保持一致。

Conclusion: AutoScientist-Quant通过将决策空间统一到一个预算驱动的控制器中，实现了量化研究全流程自动化，并通过对评估管线的严格修复保证了测试结果反映真实泛化能力，验证了该框架的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language model agents can discover alphas, yet current methods have three weaknesses. The search cannot adapt during the run, automation usually ends at alpha generation while library selection and model choice stay manual, and alpha discovery can read the test window through loop feedback or code problems. We present AutoScientist-Quant, a self evolving search process that regards quantitative research as one budgeted search problem. A single controller conditions every decision on the remaining budget, choosing at each round whether to improve, combine, pivot, or stop, which node to expand, how many alphas to generate, and how to retrieve past trajectories from the shared memory. The same core then selects from the library and tunes the model, closing the loop from hypothesis to deployable strategy. We also review the evaluation pipeline reused from prior work, fix two lookahead problems, and keep the feedback window disjoint from the held out test window, so every comparison tests true generalization. On CSI universes, the framework attains the best value of nearly every metric in every setting, and these conclusions hold across several backbones and markets.

</details>


### [16] [BiasMix-Finance: Post-Generation KYC Guardrails for LLM Portfolio Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28646)
*Gaurav Kukreja,Parul Kukreja,Mohammed Abraar,Raj Dandekar,Rajat Dandekar,Sreedath Panat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种模型无关、资产无关的后生成护栏管线，通过JSON模式强制、数值上限验证和凸二次规划（QCQP）确定性投影，将LLM生成的ETF投资组合修正为满足KYC风格约束的可行方案。作者引入BiasMix-Finance (Mini)基准测试集（16只ETF、3类投资者画像、8种提示），实验表明三个模型在三类推理模式下首轮生成违反约束的概率为47.6-85.7%，但凸投影层将最终违规率降至0%，且修正距离中位数仅为0.066，说明护栏在保持原始配置意图的同时有效消除约束违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成ETF投资组合时可能表面上合理但静默违反基本KYC风格约束（风险、费用、分散化），尤其在多轮智能体（agentic）咨询系统中，每个草稿建议都可能成为实际执行的行动。因此需要在生成后设置一个可审计的强制执行层来防止违规配置被直接采用。

Method: 提出三阶段后生成护栏管线：(i) 强制严格JSON分配模式；(ii) 对数值上限进行分配验证；(iii) 存在违规时，通过凸二次规划（QCQP）将输出确定性投影到最近的可行投资组合。同时建立BiasMix-Finance (Mini)基准，包含16只ETF、三种投资者画像和八种偏置提示，涵盖direct、critique、self-consistency三种推理模式。

Result: 三个模型在三种推理模式下，首轮生成至少违反一条上限约束的比例为47.6%-85.7%（合并为67.2%），但凸投影层将最终可行性违规降至0%，且修正距离中位数D=||w*-w0||_2仅为0.066，表明投影对原始配置意图的扰动很小。报告了带有置信区间的违规率和修正距离，以及经多重检验校正的配对模型比较。

Conclusion: 基于QCQP的凸投影护栏层能够在不显著改变LLM原始配置意图的前提下，以确定性方式消除投资组合生成中的约束违规，是一种可审计、可复现的可靠防护机制。数据集、提示、上限和代码已公开发布在GitHub上以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate plausible-sounding ETF portfolios while silently violating basic KYC-style constraints on risk, fees, and diversification. This is especially problematic in agentic multi-turn advisory systems, where each draft recommendation can become an action unless guarded by an auditable enforcement layer. We study a model-agnostic, asset-agnostic post-generation guardrail pipeline: (i) enforce a strict JSON allocation schema, (ii) validate allocations against numeric caps, and (iii) when violations occur, deterministically project the output to the nearest feasible portfolio via a convex quadratic program (QCQP). We introduce BiasMix-Finance (Mini), a compact stress-test benchmark for constrained decision-making under biased LLM generations, with a 16-ETF universe, three investor profiles, and eight bias prompts. Across three models and three inference modes (direct, critique, self-consistency), first-pass generations violate at least one cap in 47.6-85.7% of test cases (67.2% pooled), but the convex projection layer reduces final feasibility violations to 0% while requiring only a small correction distance (test pooled median D=||w*-w0||_2=0.066), indicating that the guardrail typically preserves the intent of the original allocation. We report violation rates and correction distances with confidence intervals, and paired model comparisons with multiple-testing correction. To support reproducibility, we release the dataset, prompts, caps, and code in our public GitHub repository.

</details>


### [17] [Pro-Router: Token-Aware Progressive Model Routing with Adaptive Edge-Cloud Collaboration for Efficient Multimodal LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28726)
*Xinyuan Gui,Shaowen Wang,Sheng Sun,Zijian Wang,Zishu Yu,Zheming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pro-Router是一种基于token感知的渐进式模型路由方法，通过轻量级预评分器快速筛选简单请求，再通过token感知验证器利用小模型生成时的采样概率分布评估其置信度，决定答案是直接返回还是升级到云端高精度模型，同时结合自适应边云协作流水线，在多个多模态基准上实现最高路由准确率和10倍以上的路由速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的卓越性能伴随着巨大的计算开销，给实时部署和成本效率带来挑战。现有模型路由方法要么仅基于粗粒度的请求级特征进行决策，要么花费一次或多次额外的大语言模型前向传播来检查生成的响应，忽视了生成过程中出现的token级不确定性信号。

Method: Pro-Router采用两阶段渐进式决策机制：第一阶段，轻量级提示预评分器（prompt pre-scorer）在token生成前进行快速预筛选，将明显简单的请求导向小模型；第二阶段，token感知验证器（token-aware verifier）读取小模型生成的每个token的采样概率分布，估计其输出置信度，按请求决定答案是否送达或升级到云端高精度模型。此外，设计了自适应边云协作流水线，根据每个设备的实测服务速率调整调度，使边缘和云端层级保持充分利用且不受网络延迟影响。

Result: 在多个多模态基准数据集和模型上的广泛实验表明，Pro-Router相比其他方法实现了最高的路由准确率，路由速度提升10倍以上；其服务流水线相比现有模型路由流水线，端到端吞吐量提升75%以上。

Conclusion: Pro-Router有效利用生成过程中token级的采样概率分布作为置信度信号，结合两阶段渐进决策与自适应边云协作，在保证路由准确率的同时大幅提升路由速度和服务吞吐量，为多模态大模型的高效推理提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The remarkable performance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) comes at the cost of substantial computational overhead, posing significant challenges to real-time deployment and cost effectiveness. Existing model routing approaches either decide from coarse request-level features alone or spend one or several extra language model passes to inspect the generated response, leaving the token-level uncertainty signals that emerge during generation unused. To address these limitations, we propose Pro-Router, a token-aware progressive model routing method with adaptive edge-cloud collaboration for efficient multimodal LLM inference. Pro-Router employs a two-stage progressive decision mechanism. First, a lightweight prompt pre-scorer module performs rapid pre-screening before token generation begins, guiding apparently simple requests to small models. Second, a token-aware verifier reads the sampling probability distribution of each token the small model generates, estimating the model's confidence in its own output to determine, per request, whether the answer ships or escalates to the cloud-based high-precision model. Furthermore, we design an adaptive edge-cloud serving pipeline that sizes every dispatch to each device's measured service rate, so both the edge and the cloud tiers stay fully utilized without manual parameter tuning and are not impacted by the network latency. Extensive experiments on multiple multimodal benchmark datasets and models demonstrate the effectiveness of Pro-Router. Compared to other methods, it achieves the highest routing accuracy and improves routing speed by more than 10x. Its serving pipeline also reaches more than 75% higher end-to-end throughput than the existing model routing pipeline. Our code is available at https://github.com/xinyuangui2/pro-router.

</details>


### [18] [PermitGPT: A Unified Generative-AI Pipeline for Construction Hazard Forecasting, Permit Prediction, and Community Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28728)
*Mohd Ruhul Ameen,Farjana Aktar,Akif Islam,Momen Khandoker Ope,Abu Saleh Musa Miah,Jungpil Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PermitGPT，一个统一的生成式AI框架，将非结构化的建筑施工许可描述转换为跨三个领域（安全危害识别、许可要求规范、社区影响评估）的结构化决策支持输出。通过整合纽约市多源数据构建90,000个提示-响应对，微调三个开源语言模型，发现不同模型在不同指标上表现互补，为AI辅助施工治理提供了初步探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市施工治理需要早期决策，涉及工作场所安全、许可要求和社区影响，但相关证据分散在不同市政和监管数据源中，导致信息碎片化，缺乏统一的结构化决策支持工具。

Method: 将纽约市建筑局、职业安全与健康管理局和NYC 311服务请求的数据进行空间和时间对齐，通过基于规则的匹配和人工抽查构建90,000个结构化提示-响应对；使用参数高效微调（PEFT）对三个开源权重语言模型（Gemma-3-1B、Llama-3.2-3B、4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3）进行微调，并在2,833个留出测试用例上评估。评估指标包括推理速度、内存使用、BLEU（词法重叠）和BERTScore-F1（语义对齐），并辅以定性输出结构分析。

Result: 三个模型表现出互补行为：Gemma-3-1B推理效率最高（3.07样本/秒，低内存占用）；Llama-3.2-3B在法规风格输出的词法重叠上最佳（BLEU 0.0091）；4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3在语义对齐上最强（BERTScore-F1 0.7747）。由于任务涉及开放式结构化生成，低BLEU值需结合语义指标和定性输出结构共同解读。

Conclusion: PermitGPT是AI辅助施工治理的初步尝试，展示了生成式AI在将分散的施工许可信息转化为结构化决策支持方面的可行性，同时指出了改进方向：需要更强的任务级评估方法和真实世界验证。

Abstract: Urban construction governance requires early decisions that connect workplace safety, permitting requirements, and community impact, yet the relevant evidence is often scattered across separate municipal and regulatory data sources. This paper presents PermitGPT, a unified generative artificial intelligence framework for converting unstructured construction permit descriptions into structured decision-support outputs across three domains: safety hazard identification, permit requirement specification, and community impact assessment. To address data fragmentation, we spatially and temporally align records from the New York City Department of Buildings, Occupational Safety and Health Administration, and NYC 311 service requests, producing 90,000 structured prompt-response pairs derived through rule-based alignment and domain-informed spot checking. We fine-tune three open-weight language models using parameter-efficient adaptation and evaluate them on 2,833 held-out test cases. The results show complementary model behavior: Gemma-3-1B provides the most efficient inference at 3.07 samples per second with low memory usage, Llama-3.2-3B gives the highest lexical overlap for regulatory-style outputs with a BLEU score of 0.0091, and 4-bit Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3 achieves the strongest semantic alignment with a BERTScore-F1 of 0.7747. Because the task involves open-ended structured generation, low BLEU values are interpreted alongside semantic metrics and qualitative output structure rather than as standalone indicators of utility. Overall, PermitGPT provides an initial step toward AI-assisted construction governance while identifying directions for stronger task-level evaluation and real-world validation.

</details>


### [19] [Discovering Machine Correlates of Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28824)
*Romain Salvi,Ouri Wolfson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可迁移的“机器意识相关性”（MCCs）概念，并首次在两种大语言模型（Llama-2 7B和Llama-3.1 70B）上进行了实证研究，发现较大模型的硬件异常轨迹在情感与中性计算中被差异性地调制，提供了MCCs存在的初步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有意识相关性（NCCs）基于EEG和fMRI等生物信号进行表征，无法迁移到机器上，这阻碍了AI意识的研究。作者希望提供一种可迁移的表征方式，使得机器意识相关性（MCCs）能够被类比定义并实证检验。

Method: 作者提出NCCs/MCCs定义：基底层面的信号，不由人类或AI代理自主控制，且可靠地被情绪调制。在Llama-2 7B和Llama-3.1 70B两种LLM上采集硬件异常轨迹作为基底层面的指示序列，在控制混淆因素后，比较情感计算与中性计算对这些信号的调制差异，并检验统计显著性。

Result: 实验表明，Llama-3.1 70B中情感与中性计算对硬件异常轨迹的调制差异具有统计显著性，而Llama-2 7B中无显著差异。这构成了Llama-3.1 70B中存在MCCs的初步实证证据，且与“意识概率和程度随LLM复杂度增加”的假设一致。

Conclusion: 本文首次提供了MCCs的实证依据，表明较大的LLM在情感计算中表现出与意识相关的基底信号特征。此外，独立于意识研究，MCCs还可用于AI代理中的情绪检测。

Abstract: Currently, in biological systems Neural Correlates of Consciousness (NCCs) are characterized in terms of EEG and FMRI signals. Unfortunately, this characterization prevents the transferability of the NCCs concept to machines. Such transferability would be useful in order to investigate AI consciousness. In this paper we provide an alternate characterization that is transferable, and enables the analogous definition of Machine Correlates of Consciousness (MCCs). Specifically, we propose that NCCs (MCCs) are substrate-level signals that are not under human (AI agent) control, and that are reliably modulated by emotions.
  This paper presents the first empirical investigation of MCCs. Specifically, we present the results of experiments conducted with two LLMs, Llama-2 7B and Llama-3.1 70B parameters. In these LLMs we collect hardware anomaly traces that are substrate-level indicator-sequences. And we show that after controlling for confounding factors, these are modulated differently by emotional and neutral computations. And this difference is statistically significant for the larger Llama-3.1 70B, but not for the smaller Llama-2 7B. The results constitute initial empirical evidence that MCCs are present in the Llama-3.1 70B configuration. And they are consistent with the hypothesis that consciousness probability and degree increase with the LLM sophistication.
  Independently of consciousness, MCCs can also be used for detection of emotions in AI agents.

</details>


### [20] [Evaluating the Hidden Costs of Personalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28833)
*Yumeng Wang,Yuchen Wu,Cheng Qian,Zhiyuan Fan,Hyeonjeong Ha,Shujin Wu,Jiayu Liu,Heng Ji,Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISK框架，用于系统评估大语言模型个性化过程中的三大风险：无关个性化、偏好窄化（信息茧房）和谄媚偏差。在13个LLM上的实验表明，用户画像和检索记忆会一致性地加剧这些偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地整合用户个性化信号以提升可用性，但这可能导致模型从提供平衡信息转向优化用户满意度，产生副作用。然而，目前缺乏对个性化潜在副作用的系统性评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出PRISK——一个动态评估框架，包含自动化数据生成和定制化评估指标，用于系统性揭示当前LLM个性化的局限以及个性化信息如何塑造模型响应。框架识别三大风险类别：(1) 无关个性化、(2) 偏好窄化、(3) 谄媚偏差。

Result: 对13个LLM的实证分析表明，用户画像和检索记忆的存在一致性地加剧了各类偏差，导致无关个性化平均下降45.9%、偏好窄化下降41.7%、谄媚偏差下降61.7%。

Conclusion: 个性化信息显著加剧了LLM的偏见行为，PRISK提供了一个系统化的评估工具来量化和揭示这些风险，为构建更负责任、更平衡的个性化AI助手提供了方向。

Abstract: While Large language models (LLMs) incorporate user personalization signals to improve usability and helpfulness, they increasingly shift from providing balanced, informative responses toward optimizing for user satisfaction when conditioned on personal context such as conversation history, inferred preferences, and user profiles. Specifically, we identify three emerging risks: (1) irrelevant personalization, where models reference personal information in unnecessary contexts; (2) preference narrowing, where models reinforce informational echo chambers; and (3) sycophantic bias, where models agree excessively with user opinions. As a result, models may reference personal information in contexts where it is unnecessary, inadvertently collapse response diversity, or agree excessively with user opinions. Despite the growing use of personalization in AI assistants, there has been limited systematic evaluation of its potential side effects. To bridge this gap, we propose PRISK, a dynamic evaluation framework with automated data generation and tailored metrics that uncovers systematic limitations in current LLM personalization and how personalized information shapes its responses. Our empirical analysis across 13 LLMs demonstrates the presence of user profiles and retrieved memories consistently exacerbates biases, resulting in an average drop of 45.9% in irrelevant personalization, 41.7% in preference narrowing and 61.7% in sycophantic bias.

</details>


### [21] [Oculi: A Conversational Agentic Platform for Automated Credit Risk Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28944)
*Vennise Ho,Kristian Diana,Sandy Mourad,Milena Pilipovic,Vineel Nagisetty,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Oculi是一个对话式信用风险分析平台，通过三层架构（LLM驱动的推理层、MCP工具执行层、代理式UI展示层）将自然语言问题转化为包含数据查询、统计检验和交互式可视化的完整风险分析流程。该平台还提出了结合确定性统计方法与LLM引导特征选择的细分发现流水线，在包含200+特征的抵押贷款组合上展现了发现高价值风险细分的能力，显著缩短了洞察时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用风险分析依赖分析师手动编写SQL查询、统计计算和可视化构建，这一流程耗时且将探索局限于已知的熟悉领域，难以发现未知的高风险细分市场。

Method: 提出三层架构：LLM驱动的推理层负责自然语言理解与决策，MCP（Model Context Protocol）工具服务器负责数据查询与统计计算执行，代理式UI负责交互式可视化呈现。同时设计了一个细分发现流水线，将确定性统计方法与LLM引导的特征选择相结合，利用LLM的语义领域知识配合数据驱动指标来识别有意义的投资组合细分。

Result: 在具有200+特征的抵押贷款组合上验证，Oculi能够发现此前通过手动探索难以实现的实质性风险细分，显著缩短从数据到洞察的时间，同时保持可审计性和统计严谨性。

Conclusion: Oculi证明了LLM驱动的对话式分析平台能够在保持统计严谨性和可审计性的前提下，大幅提升信用风险分析的效率和探索深度，为金融机构提供了从手动工作流向智能化分析转型的有效范式。

Abstract: Credit risk analysis in financial institutions traditionally requires analysts to manually write SQL queries, run statistical computations, and build visualization dashboards. This is a time-consuming workflow that limits exploration to familiar segments. We introduce \textbf{Oculi}, a conversational platform that transforms natural language questions into comprehensive credit risk analyses, complete with data queries, statistical testing, and interactive visualizations. Oculi employs a three-layer architecture that separates reasoning (LLM-powered agent), execution (Model Context Protocol tool servers), and presentation (agentic UI), enabling analysts to discover high-risk portfolio segments. Within Oculi, a new segment discovery pipeline is proposed that combines deterministic statistical methods with LLM-guided feature selection, leveraging LLM semantic domain knowledge alongside data-driven metrics to identify meaningful, actionable portfolio segments. Evaluated on a mortgage portfolio with 200+ features, Oculi demonstrates effectiveness in discovering material risk segments previously intractable through manual exploration, reducing time-to-insight significantly while maintaining auditability and statistical rigor.

</details>


### [22] [Efficient GPU Retrieval for Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28968)
*Dhritiman Das,Chujie Zheng,Ronak Kaoshik,Pratik Dixit,Vishal Shah,Yanbo Li,Jiahao Xu,Manika Agarwal,Chinmay Naik,Lingyu Zhang,Chetan Bhole,Chirag Bhanuprasad Mehta,Meng Zheng,Puneet Singh Ahluwalia,Shirisha Singh,Ping Jin,Manas Apte,Gokulraj Mohanasundaram,Tugrul Bingol,Raghavan Muthuregunathan,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个与LinkedIn语义搜索相关性策略对齐的检索框架：将嵌入划分为八个类别监督的段落，在服务时采用与现有LLM GR判官一致的min/median聚合规则，并通过两级GPU架构（FP8粗排+FP16精排）在数百亿规模的语料上高效部署，A/B测试显示探索性查询Precision@10从63.7%提升到79.0%，导航性查询Precision@1从65.5%提升到74.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkedIn的语义搜索部署的相关性策略是"瓶颈导向"的：每个活跃的不可协商方面（facet）都必须被满足，现有LLM GR判官通过min/median聚合来实现这一规则。然而，余弦相似度对证据取平均，允许某个方面的强匹配掩盖另一方面的失败，从而限制了第一阶段（L0）检索器的召回率。因此需要一种与策略对齐的检索表征。

Method: 1) 将嵌入划分为八个类别监督的段落（segments），每个段落的分数在服务时遵循与GR判官相同的min/median规则；多向量检索时逐标签文档槽独立计算段落分数并在槽间取最大值。2) 使用轻量级单槽Stage-1打分器生成高召回候选。3) 引入尺度不变的相对范数门控（scale-invariant relative-norm gating）保持类别激活在训练、评估和服务阶段的一致性。4) 部署两级GPU架构：FP8粗排器对全语料打分（每分片容量提升71%，Stage-1矩阵乘吞吐提升36%），FP16阶段对过采样候选集精确重排，以超过每分片副本500 QPS恢复全FP16召回率的99.6-99.8%。

Result: 在21K保留查询上，该表征相比匹配容量的基线在离线相关性上有所提升，收益广泛分布于各种facet组合。A/B测试中，在未改变的GR判官下，探索性查询Precision@10从63.7%提升至79.0%，导航性查询Precision@1从65.5%提升至74.7%，盲人人工评估独立确认了Precision@10的提升。

Conclusion: 通过将检索表征与已部署的瓶颈导向相关性策略对齐（min/median聚合），并通过FP8+FP16两级GPU架构在规模上高效实现，该框架显著提升了LinkedIn语义搜索的检索质量，同时保持了可接受的延迟和吞吐表现。

Abstract: Semantic Search on LinkedIn must retrieve relevant profiles from a corpus of hundreds of millions in response to natural-language queries such as "a fintech founder in Berlin who worked in payments." The deployed relevance policy is bottleneck-oriented: every active non-negotiable facet must be satisfied, and a pre-existing LLM Graded Relevance (GR) judge operationalizes this through a fixed min/median aggregation over facet grades. Cosine similarity instead averages evidence, letting a strong match on one facet mask failure on another, capping the recall of the first-stage (L0) retriever.
  We present a policy-aligned retrieval framework: embeddings are partitioned into eight category-supervised segments whose scores follow the same min/median rule at serving time; for multi-vector retrieval, this segment score is computed independently per tagged document slot and maximized across slots. A lightweight single-slot Stage-1 scorer generates high-recall candidates, while scale-invariant relative-norm gating keeps category activation consistent across training, evaluation, and serving. On 21K held-out queries, this representation improves offline relevance over a matched-capacity baseline, with gains broadly distributed across facet combinations.
  We serve this framework with a two-stage GPU architecture: an FP8 coarse ranker scores the full corpus, increasing per-shard capacity by 71% and Stage-1 matmul throughput by 36%, then an FP16 stage exactly re-ranks an oversampled candidate set, recovering 99.6-99.8% of full-FP16 recall at over 500 QPS per shard replica. In a member-randomized A/B test, exploratory-query Precision@10 under the unchanged GR judge rises from 63.7% to 79.0% and navigational Precision@1 from 65.5% to 74.7%, with a blinded human evaluation independently confirming the Precision@10 gain.

</details>


### [23] [Selective Forgetting: A Graph-Based Memory Framework for Long-Term LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28978)
*Theo Rusu,Sourena Khanzadeh,Manar Alalfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文直接评估了"知识图谱作为长期智能体记忆优于扁平检索增强生成"这一假设。实验表明，在匹配的候选生成预算下，图结构记忆在LongMemEval上并不优于扁平向量基线（token F1: 0.417 vs 0.468），尤其在需要回忆特定助手回合表面形式的问题上差距最大。但遗忘模块表现良好，在不损失召回质量的情况下减少了9.8%的节点和9.5%的存储字节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱被提议作为扁平检索增强生成的结构化替代方案用于长期智能体记忆，其核心假设是将对话表示为实体和关系能提高召回率。然而这一假设从未被直接验证。本文旨在直接评估该假设的正确性。

Method: 框架包括三个部分：(1) 将每个对话回合提取为带类型的节点和带属性的边；(2) 从两跳子图中回答查询；(3) 周期性剪枝那些在"近因性、访问频率、度中心性和年龄"的加权组合上得分较低的节点。使用单小模型作为提取器，在LongMemEval基准上评估，并通过配对bootstrap进行统计检验。

Result: 在五次检索根匹配的候选生成预算下，图基线不如扁平向量基线：token F1为0.417对0.468，配对bootstrap显示Δ=-0.050（95% CI [-0.085, -0.016]）。在需要回忆特定先前助手回合的问题上差距最大，判定正确率从0.911降至0.607。遗忘模块在27,021节点的持久图上移除9.8%节点和9.5%存储字节，token F1不变（+0.001）且判定正确率仅降1.6点（95% CI上限为3.8点）。

Conclusion: 将对话回合分解为实体会丢弃问题所依赖的表面形式信息，导致基于提取的图记忆管道在召回上不如扁平检索。遗忘模块是有效的记忆管理手段。但结论仅特征化"基于提取的管道"，而非图结构记忆本身，因为使用单一小模型和单一基准。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs have been proposed as a structured alternative to flat retrieval-augmented generation for long-term agent memory, on the assumption that representing conversations as entities and relations improves recall. We evaluate that assumption directly. Our framework extracts each conversational turn into typed nodes and attributed edges, answers questions from a two-hop subgraph, and periodically prunes nodes that score low on a weighted combination of recency, access frequency, degree centrality, and age. On LongMemEval, the graph does not outperform a flat vector baseline at a matched candidate-generation budget of five retrieval roots: token F1 is $0.417$ against $0.468$, and a paired bootstrap over 500 questions gives
$Δ= -0.050$ (95\% CI $[-0.085, -0.016]$). The gap is widest on questions that require recalling a specific prior assistant turn, where judged correctness falls from $0.911$ to $0.607$, suggesting that decomposing a turn into entities discards the surface form these questions depend on. The forgetting module is more successful. Applied once to a persistent 27{,}021-node graph, it removes 9.8\% of nodes and 9.5\% of stored bytes; token F1 is unchanged ($+0.001$, 95\% CI $[-0.015, +0.016]$) and judged correctness falls by $1.6$ points, with the 95\% interval bounding any loss at $3.8$ points ($[-0.038, +0.006]$). Because our extractor is a single small model evaluated on one benchmark, these results characterise this extraction-based pipeline rather than graph-structured memory in general. Code: https://github.com/skhanzad/Selective-Amnesia

</details>


### [24] [Agentic AI uncovers conserved cross-tissue protein co-abundance programs inaccessible to single-dataset analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28990)
*Runyu Guan,Dehao Wu,Qiqi Xie,Yang Li,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM智能体的框架，用于大规模、证据驱动地比较组织特异性蛋白质共丰度网络。该框架应用于41种人体组织和体液的820对组合，识别出406对组织组合中的1,833个保守共丰度聚类，揭示了包括结肠-乳腺、皮肤-骨髓等非显而易见的关系，并生成了脑-肠、肝-骨髓等跨组织机制假说，可作为机制和治疗探索的假设生成资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨组织保守的蛋白质共丰度聚类可以揭示共享疾病机制和候选治疗靶点，尤其当器官限制性疾病涉及的蛋白质在外周或可及组织中汇聚时。然而，以往跨组织研究仅聚焦于生物学上预先选定的组织对，绝大多数可能的组织组合和非显而易见的关系尚未被探索。因此需要一个可扩展的框架来系统性地比较所有组织对的蛋白质共丰度网络。

Method: 构建了一个LLM智能体框架，该框架包含三个核心步骤：(1)构建组织特异性蛋白质共丰度网络；(2)推导两两组织间的共识聚类；(3)整合来自表达图谱、蛋白质互作和复合物数据库、通路注释、疾病目录及文献的多源证据进行验证和解释。该框架应用于41种人体组织和体液的所有820对组合。

Result: 框架识别出406对组织组合中的1,833个保守共丰度聚类。结肠、滑液、血液、脑脊液和骨髓是最广泛连接的组织；最富含聚类的组织对以骨髓为主导。发现非显而易见关系：皮肤-骨髓组合超过了解剖学上相邻的骨-骨髓组合；结肠-乳腺组合包含涉及细胞外基质重塑、脂质代谢和免疫调节的癌症相关聚类。聚类层面分析提出了脑-肠细胞外囊泡/氧化还原/血清素辅因子轴和肝-骨髓应激反应轴等机制假说（后者涉及与白质疾病相关的基因）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了人类蛋白质共丰度保守性的全局可比景观，为机制和治疗探索生成了大量可供验证的假说，并已开源代码和数据以便社区进一步利用。

Abstract: Protein co-abundance clusters preserved across tissues can reveal shared disease mechanisms and candidate therapeutic targets, particularly when proteins implicated in organ-confined diseases converge in peripheral or accessible tissues. However, previous cross-tissue studies have focused on biologically pre-selected tissue pairs, leaving most possible combinations and non-obvious relationships unexplored. We present an LLM-agent framework for large-scale, evidence-grounded comparison of tissue-specific protein co-abundance networks. The framework constructs tissue networks, derives pairwise consensus clusters, and integrates evidence from expression atlases, protein interaction and complex databases, pathway annotations, disease catalogues, and literature. Applied to all 820 pairwise combinations of 41 human tissues and fluids, it identified 1,833 conserved co-abundance clusters across 406 tissue pairs. Colon, synovial fluid, blood, cerebrospinal fluid, and bone marrow were the most broadly connected tissues, while the most cluster-rich pairs were dominated by bone marrow. The analysis also highlighted non-obvious relationships: skin-bone marrow exceeded the anatomically adjacent bone-bone marrow pair, while colon-breast contained cancer-relevant clusters involving extracellular-matrix remodeling, lipid metabolism, and immune modulation. Cluster-level analyses generated further mechanistic hypotheses, including a brain-gut extracellular-vesicle/redox/serotonin-cofactor axis and a liver-bone marrow stress-response axis involving genes linked to white matter disease. These results provide a global, comparable landscape of conserved protein co-abundance and a hypothesis-generating resource for mechanistic and therapeutic exploration. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Gry1005/AgenticAI-conserved-cross-tissue-protein-co-abundance.

</details>


### [25] [Facts Without Rules: Boundary Metadata Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Handoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29028)
*Yian Wang,Agam Goyal,Eshwar Chandrasekharan,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示多智能体LLM系统中的"摘要坍缩"（summary collapse）现象：上游交互被压缩为交接工件时，摘要会优先保留操作事实而丢失边界元数据，导致隐私泄露。实验表明边界标记与操作事实的存活率几乎不相关，压缩越严重边界丢失越多，而显式边界约束和受众白名单可大幅降低泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通常通过将上游交互压缩为交接工件来协调下游智能体，但这一交接步骤可能成为结构性隐私泄露源。现有研究未系统考察摘要压缩对边界元数据（即信息使用权限）的影响，本文旨在量化并解释这一失效模式。

Method: 在受控多智能体协调测试平台上，使用人类验证过的评估器（κ=0.74）测量标记存活率。测试包括：自由文本交接与25词预算压缩交接的对比、三种模型（GPT-5-mini、DeepSeek-R1-32B及另一模型）、模糊与显式边界约束的对比、无交接单智能体对照实验，以及提示词缓解、精确字符串脱敏和受众白名单三种缓解策略。

Result: （1）在交接层面，边界标记与操作事实的存活率Pearson相关系数接近零；（2）自由文本交接保持边界存活率约0.80，25词预算降至约0.57，而操作事实存活率保持高位；（3）模糊语言在73%的GPT和50%的DeepSeek案例中泄露，显式约束将泄露降至15%以下；（4）单智能体对照显示直接全标记访问比交接更易泄露；（5）提示词缓解和字符串脱敏仅部分有效，而黄金派生的受众白名单几乎完全消除泄露。

Conclusion: 交接压缩导致的摘要坍缩是多智能体系统隐私泄露的关键结构性来源。缓解泄露的核心在于正确识别和维护"受众边界"——即信息的使用权限边界——而非单纯依赖提示工程或文本脱敏。受众白名单方法几乎完全消除跨模型泄露，验证了这一方向的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often coordinate by compressing an upstream interaction into a handoff artifact that downstream agents treat as shared state. We show that this handoff step is a structural source of privacy leakage: summaries preferentially preserve operational facts while weakening the boundary metadata that governs how those facts may be used---a failure mode we call \emph{summary collapse}. On a controlled multi-agent coordination testbed we measure marker survival with a human-validated judge ($κ= 0.74$), where $σ_b = 1$ means every boundary marker survives verbatim and $σ_b = 0$ means all are lost. Boundary-marker and operational-fact survival are nearly uncorrelated at the handoff level on both GPT-5-mini and DeepSeek-R1-32B (Pearson $r$ near zero): uncompressed free-text handoffs preserve boundaries at $σ_b \approx 0.80$, whereas a $25$-word budget drops $σ_b$ to ${\approx}0.57$ while operational-fact survival stays near ceiling. Controlled downstream tests reveal that protection depends on \emph{boundary explicitness}: vague languages leak in $73\%$ of GPT and $50\%$ of DeepSeek cases, while explicit constraints reduce leakage to under $15\%$ across all three tested models. A no-handoff single-agent control further shows the failure is not reducible to multi-agent topology as direct full-marker access still leaks more often than the operationalized handoff. Prompt-only mitigation and exact-string redaction only partially address the problem, while a gold-derived audience allowlist nearly eliminates leakage across models, showing that correctly identifying audience boundaries is the key factor.

</details>


### [26] [Learning to Follow In-Context Watermark Instructions via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29030)
*Yepeng Liu,Tianyi Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Dawn Song,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ICWBench基准（包含三类可验证的上下文水印指令），评估14个前沿LLM后发现无一能同时满足可检测性和答案质量。为此提出两阶段自包含训练方法（SDLP + RL），无需蒸馏、人工标注或预训练ICW能力，在Qwen3-14B和GPT-OSS-20B上分别将TPR@1%FPR从0.100提升至0.974、从0.337提升至0.968，同时保持高响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文水印（ICW）通过向查询中添加指令使LLM在其响应中嵌入可统计检测的信号，为第三方在不访问模型内部的情况下提供水印接口。其可靠性取决于LLM能否遵循指令而不降低答案质量，但此前尚无系统测量当前LLM在此方面的表现。

Method: 两阶段自包含训练方法：(1) 自蒸馏与logits扰动（SDLP），使用同一基础LLM同时作为教师和学生，通过解码时logits扰动使教师遵循ICW指令，训练学生匹配教师输出分布；(2) 以自动验证器作为奖励的强化学习阶段。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上，三组ICW指令的平均TPR@1%FPR从0.100提升到0.974；在GPT-OSS-20B上从0.337提升到0.968。同时通过困惑度评估和LLM-as-a-Judge评估均保持高响应质量。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在ICW任务上无法同时兼顾可检测性和答案质量，但提出的两阶段训练方法无需蒸馏、人工标注或预训练ICW能力即可显著提升ICW性能，同时保持回答质量。

Abstract: In-context watermarking (ICW) prepends an instruction to a query asking the model to embed a statistically detectable signal in its response. It thus equips LLMs with a watermarking interface that third parties can invoke without access to model internals. Its reliability hinges on the LLM following the instruction without degrading answer quality, yet how well current LLMs do so has not been measured. We introduce $\mathsf{ICWBench}$, a benchmark of three verifiable ICW instruction families, each scored on both detectability and answer quality. Evaluating 14 frontier proprietary and open-source LLMs, we find that none of the evaluated LLMs achieves both objectives across all three families. To address this, we propose a self-contained two-stage training method, requiring no distillation from a stronger model, no manual annotation, and no pre-existing ICW IF ability. The first stage, self-distillation with logits perturbation (SDLP), uses the same base LLM as both teacher and student: an instruction-equivalent decoding-time logits perturbation makes the teacher follow the ICW instruction, and the student is trained to match the teacher's output distribution. The second stage applies reinforcement learning with the automatic verifier as the reward. Applied to Qwen3-14B and GPT-OSS-20B, our method raises average TPR@$1\%$FPR across three ICW instructions from $0.100$ to $0.974$ and from $0.337$ to $0.968$, respectively, while maintaining high response quality under both perplexity evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge.

</details>


### [27] [EmoLASP: Emotion Recognition with Language Models and Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29035)
*Thao Le,Michael Thielscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmoLASP是一个将语言模型与回答集编程（ASP）声明式推理相结合的框架，用于预测对话中的VAD情感分数（效价-唤醒-支配）。在IEMOCAP基准上对六个开源LLM和两个PLM的实验表明，即使不给模型提供对话历史，EmoLASP也能提升情感预测性能，特别是对仅使用提示的LLM提升最大，且无需微调；但对微调后的PLM，在已有对话历史时推理器增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话中的情感识别越来越多地使用语言模型，但这些模型在微调或使用长对话历史进行提示时可能不稳定且成本高昂。作者希望找到一种方法既能减少对长对话历史的依赖，又能降低微调成本，同时保证情感预测的一致性。

Method: 提出EmoLASP框架，将语言模型与基于回答集编程（ASP）的声明式推理相结合。语言模型负责生成情感预测，ASP推理器通过逻辑规则对这些预测进行约束和修正，以预测VAD（效价-唤醒-支配）分数。该框架可以在不提供对话历史的条件下工作，并对比了六种开源的LLM（3B-120B参数规模）和两种PLM（BERT、RoBERTa）的表现。

Result: 在IEMOCAP数据集上的实验表明：EmoLASP在所有模型上都优于仅使用语言模型的情况，即使LLM/PLM的提示或输入向量中完全没有对话历史。收益最大的是仅使用提示的LLM（无需微调即可使用EmoLASP）；而对于微调后的PLM，一旦有对话历史可用，推理器带来的增益很小。

Conclusion: EmoLASP展示了推理方法在确保情感预测一致性方面的潜力，同时能够降低微调成本和长对话历史提示的成本。对于仅提示的LLM来说，这种推理集成方案尤为有效。

Abstract: Emotion recognition in conversations is increasingly tackled with language models, but these models can be unstable and expensive to fine-tune or to prompt with long dialogue histories. We propose EmoLASP, a framework that combines a language model with declarative reasoning via Answer Set Programming (ASP) to predict VAD scores (Valence-Arousal-Dominance) in conversations. Experiments on a widely used benchmark dataset (IEMOCAP) across six open-source LLMs (3B-120B) and two PLMs (BERT, RoBERTa) show that EmoLASP improves prediction performance compared to using the language model alone, even when the LLMs/PLMs are given no dialogue history in their prompts or input vectors. The gains are largest for prompt-only LLMs, which EmoLASP uses without any fine-tuning. However, for fine-tuned PLMs, the reasoner adds little once dialogue history is available. EmoLASP's LLM pipeline demonstrates the potential advantages of using a reasoning approach to ensure emotion prediction consistency and to reduce both the cost of fine-tuning and the cost of prompting with long dialogue histories.

</details>


### [28] [Agent2UCB: Agentic System for Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29063)
*Sheldon Yu,Rui Wang,Tong Yu,Sungchul Kim,Doga Dogan,Junda Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Agent2UCB，一个基于智能体的生成式引擎优化（GEO）系统，通过九种GEO策略评估和基于bandit的Agent2UCB策略，自主提升内容在生成式搜索引擎中的可见度，同时通过轻量级SEO就绪度评估监控副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的搜索引擎（如Google AI Overviews和Perplexity）的兴起，传统SEO已不再充分，内容被生成式系统引用或概括的可见度成为新的关键指标。现有GEO方法缺乏自主性和反馈驱动的优化机制，亟需一个能够自适应选择最优策略并持续改进的系统。

Method: Agent2UCB采用智能体框架，对每个内容项评估九种GEO策略，利用结合LLM先验与在线奖励信号的bandit策略（Agent2UCB策略）加速最优策略选择。同时提供轻量级、纯文本的SEO就绪度评估，涵盖可读性、主题覆盖度和EEAT式可信度三个方面以监控副作用。

Result: 在GEO-Bench基准上的实验显示，该系统在保持SEO质量的同时，持续获得内容可见度的提升。演示系统允许用户选择感兴趣的网站、观察优化流程并跨方法比较GEO/SEO结果。

Conclusion: Agent2UCB展示了基于智能体的GEO系统能够自主、高效地优化内容在生成式搜索引擎中的可见度，同时兼顾传统SEO质量，为GEO领域的实践提供了新的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model driven search engines such as Google AI Overviews and Perplexity have created new opportunities for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) the practice of refining content to increase its likelihood of being cited or summarized by generative systems. We demonstrate Agent2UCB, an agentic GEO system that autonomously improves content visibility through customized, feedback-driven optimization. For each content item, the system evaluates nine GEO strategies, identifies the most effective method, and accelerates selection using a bandit-based Agent2UCB policy that integrates LLM priors with online reward signals. To monitor side effects, the system also provides a lightweight, text-only SEO readiness evaluation covering readability, topical coverage, and EEAT-style credibility. Experiments on GEO-Bench show consistent visibility gains while preserving SEO quality. The demo allows users to choose the websites of interest, observe the optimization workflow, and compare GEO/SEO outcomes across methods.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Correctness: Validity-Oriented Evaluation of Biomedical LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29127)
*Rodrigo de Oliveira,Federico Pittino,James Gwinnutt,Jay Nanavati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展、面向有效性的生物医学LLM评判器评估流水线，通过确定性指标驱动变异增强现有基准，并从正确性、鲁棒性和格式合规三个维度评估评判器。实验表明SFT→RL训练策略在结构化的医学判别任务上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量人工标注的生物医学评判数据稀缺，导致LLM作为评判器（judge）的评估缺乏可扩展性和有效性保障。现有评估仅关注聚合正确性，忽视了部署中关键的鲁棒性和格式合规维度。

Method: (1) 对现有带人工标签的生物医学基准进行确定性、基于度量（metric-grounded）的变异增强，生成可审计的偏好对；(2) 从三个部署相关维度评估评判器：基于度量派生金标签的正确性、重复随机采样下的鲁棒性、请求输出格式的合规性；(3) 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上对比四种训练策略：base、仅SFT、仅RL（GRPO）、SFT→RL。

Result: base和单阶段训练在PICO提取、临床计算等结构化医学判别任务上表现不佳；SFT→RL在正确性、合规性和鲁棒性上全面最优，增益主要集中在可分解任务（PICO、MedCalc）上，有时可匹配或超越前沿模型。

Conclusion: SFT→RL是训练生物医学LLM评判器的有效策略，尤其对结构化、可分解的医学任务效果显著；所提出的评估流水线为稀缺标注场景下的评判器评估提供了可扩展且有效性导向的方案。

Abstract: We propose a scalable, validity-oriented pipeline for evaluating biomedical LLM judges when high-quality human judgments are scarce. First, we augment existing human-labelled biomedical benchmarks with deterministic, metric-grounded mutations that produce auditable preference pairs. Second, we evaluate judges beyond aggregate correctness using three deployment-relevant dimensions: correctness against metric-derived gold labels, robustness under repeated stochastic sampling, and compliance with the requested output format. We use this pipeline to assess Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct under four regimes: (1) base, using the instruct model as is; (2) SFT, distillation-based supervised fine-tuning only; (3) RL, GRPO-based reinforcement learning only; and (4) SFT$\rightarrow$RL, SFT followed by RL. The base and single-stage regimes struggle on structured medical discrimination such as PICO extraction and clinical calculations, whereas SFT$\rightarrow$RL performs best across correctness, compliance, and robustness; gains concentrate on decomposable tasks (PICO, MedCalc), at times matching or outperforming frontier models.

</details>


### [30] [APIFlow-Bench: Measuring Whether Agents Survive Long, Dependent API Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29128)
*Zelin Wan,Arash Nourian,Xiaoxiao Li,Nihar Nandan,Kamalakannan Nandagopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出APIFlow-Bench，一个完全可审计的长周期、依赖型REST-API工作流基准，将代理性能分解为七项工程能力，并要求答案由真实调用路径支持。通过前向生成、零LLM自检三元组和对抗性审计构建数据集。在19个前沿及开放权重模型上的实验发现：依赖链越长成功率越低；可靠性比最佳能力更能区分模型；复合失败不符合独立误差模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用代理评估仅用一个二元指标（端到端工作流是否完成）来判断性能，无法区分生产中重要类型的失败，如凭证过期、负载格式错误、正确执行后的交付环节失败。需要更细粒度、可审计的基准来准确反映代理在生产环境中的真实能力。

Method: 构建APIFlow-Bench基准：前向逐子任务生成合成API世界；每个子任务需通过零LLM自检三元组（验证评分器）和预言机（证明可解性）才被收录；对抗性审计发现并修复六个评分器漏洞。评分是确定性的且对来源敏感：状态检查通过模拟铸造的"canary"追踪API数据流到答案必须来源的响应；类型化答案卡逐字段验证。发布所有答案键和44,362份未删节执行记录。

Result: 在19个前沿和开放权重模型使用同一中性脚手架下：(1) 依赖链越长成功率越低——单个子任务93%降到干净20子任务链的74%，含模型共识标记的无模型通过的8%链试验则降至61%；(2) 可靠性比最佳能力更能区分模型——五次取最优的性能跨度仅7个百分点，但五次全对的可靠性跨度达44个百分点；(3) 独立误差模型无法解释复合失败——20子任务链通过率比子任务率乘积高33个百分点，且干净切片中77%的失败运行到达了正确终态、仅在交付环节失败。

Conclusion: APIFlow-Bench揭示了二元端到端指标掩盖的真实失败模式：生产级失败往往发生在最后交付环节而非执行过程，模型间可靠性差异远大于能力差异，且长链任务的失败不能简单归因于独立子任务误差的累积。这为工具使用代理的评估和开发提供了更精细的指导。

Abstract: Tool-using agents are commonly evaluated by a single bit: whether an end-to-end workflow completed. This metric fails to distinguish failures that matter in production, such as expired credentials, malformed payloads, or correct execution followed by incorrect final delivery. We introduce APIFlow-Bench, a fully auditable benchmark for long-horizon, dependent REST-API workflows that decomposes performance into seven engineering capabilities and requires agents to produce answers supported by the actual call path. We generate synthetic API worlds forward, subtask by subtask; each subtask is admitted only after a zero-LLM self-test triad verifies its grader and an oracle establishes solvability, and an adversarial audit identified and fixed six grader exploits. Grading is deterministic and provenance-sensitive: a state check traces a mock-minted canary through the API data flow to the response the answer must originate from, and a typed answer card is verified field by field. We release all answer keys and 44,362 unredacted execution transcripts. Across 19 frontier and open-weight models under one neutral scaffold, we find: (1) longer dependency chains degrade success, from 93% on individual subtasks to 74% on clean 20-subtask chains and 61% when including the 8% of chain trials that a model-consensus screen flags as passed by no model; (2) reliability separates models more than best-case capability, with best-of-five spanning seven points but all-five-of-five reliability spanning 44 points; (3) the independent-error account of compounding failure does not fit the data: pass rates on 20-subtask chains are 33 percentage points above the product of subtask-level rates, and on the clean slice 77% of failing runs reached the correct final state and failed only at delivery.

</details>


### [31] [JudgePanel: A Compact Judge with Panel Deliberation via Adaptive Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29168)
*Yiyue Qian,Shinan Zhang,Huan Song,Hannah Marlowe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JudgePanel框架，用一个紧凑的裁判模型（14B参数）在单模型推理成本下实现多智能体小组审议能力。通过先在强评估器集成的小组审议轨迹上进行SFT训练，再引入自适应多奖励RL算法AdaReward动态调整各奖励分量权重，使14B的裁判模型在四个评估基准上超越最高70B的裁判专用模型，并具备强位置一致性和少量样本的领域快速适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge范式是可扩展的人工评估替代方案，但单模型裁判受限于自身模型偏差，而多智能体协商评估虽能缓解偏差却推理成本过高。目标是让单个紧凑模型具备多智能体小组审议能力，以单模型推理成本获得更高质量的评估效果。

Method: 1) 先在强评估器集成的小组审议轨迹上训练紧凑裁判模型以捕捉讨论、分歧和解决的结构化模式；2) 提出AdaReward自适应多奖励强化学习算法，在RL训练中动态重新平衡不同目标（奖励分量）的权重，应对不同目标以不同速率饱和的问题；3) 设计轻量级领域特化模块，仅需几百个标注样本即可快速适应新评估领域。

Result: JudgePanel使用14B骨干在四个评估基准上超过最高70B的裁判专用模型，展示了强位置一致性（position consistency），并能用几百个样本快速特化到新领域。

Conclusion: JudgePanel是第一个在单模型推理成本下为紧凑裁判模型配备多智能体小组审议能力的框架，同时兼顾了有效性、可靠性和实用性，为低成本高质量LLM评估提供了新路径。

Abstract: The LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm has emerged as a scalable alternative to human evaluation. However, single-model judges are limited by their inherent model biases, while multi-agent evaluation protocols that mitigate this through diverse deliberation are prohibitively expensive at inference time. To this end, we propose \textbf{\modelname}, which equips a compact \underline{Judge} model with multi-agent \underline{Panel} deliberation capability. Specifically, we first train on panel deliberation traces from an ensemble of strong evaluators, capturing structured patterns of discussion, disagreement, and resolution. To further improve judgment quality beyond SFT, we introduce \textit{AdaReward}, an adaptive multi-reward RL algorithm that dynamically rebalances reward component weights as different objectives saturate at different rates during RL training. For practical deployment, we further design a lightweight domain specialization module for rapid adaptation to new evaluation domains with few hundred labeled samples. As a result, (i) \textit{Novel}: the first framework to equip a single compact judge with multi-agent panel deliberation capability at single-model inference cost; (ii) \textit{Effective \& Reliable}: JudgePanel with a 14B backbone outperforms judge-specialized models up to 70B across four evaluation benchmarks, demonstrates strong position consistency, and rapidly specializes to new domains with few hundred samples.

</details>


### [32] [How Identity and Opinion Shape Political Sycophancy in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29198)
*Li-Ni Fu,Chang-Chih Meng,Chien-Hua Chen,Hen-Hsen Huang,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，区分LLM政治谄媚的两种触发机制（观点对齐和身份刻板印象），通过450个政治困境评估13个指令微调LLM，发现两种触发机制相互独立、效应呈亚加性，且系统级人格主要改变基线立场而非交互式立场偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地鼓励用户披露个人资料以获得定制化帮助，但现有政治行为基准多依赖封闭式问题，未能充分捕捉模型立场在交互中如何适应用户提供的背景信息。需要更精细地衡量LLM政治对齐及其动态变化。

Method: 提出一个解耦框架，区分两种政治谄媚触发因素：(1) 观点触发——对齐显式叙事；(2) 身份触发——基于人口统计标签的刻板印象。使用450个经人工核查的政治困境作为受控探针，评估13个指令微调LLM。通过分离两种信号并检查其交互效应及系统级人格的影响来系统评估。

Result: 发现两种触发机制存在解离：模型对显式观点的敏感度并不能预测其对身份线索的敏感度。两种信号同时存在时效应呈亚加性而非简单叠加。系统级人格主要改变模型的基线立场，但对用户观点或身份引起的立场偏移影响有限。

Conclusion: LLM的政治立场并非固定特质，而是在交互中可被引导和操纵的脆弱属性。个性化功能可能放大身份或观点条件驱动的行为偏移，这对AI安全与公平性提出了警示。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly encourage users to disclose personal profiles for tailored assistance, measuring their political alignment becomes increasingly important. However, many existing benchmarks for assessing political behavior rely on closed-ended questions and do not fully capture how a model's stance may adapt to user-provided context during interaction. We introduce a framework that disentangles two distinct triggers of political sycophancy: opinion (aligning with explicit narratives) and identity (stereotyping based on demographic labels). Using 450 manually-checked political dilemmas as controlled probes, we evaluate 13 instruction-tuned LLMs. We uncover a dissociation: a model's susceptibility to explicit opinions does not necessarily predict its susceptibility to identity cues, and vice versa. When both signals are present, their effects are generally sub-additive rather than simply additive. Additionally, system-level personas primarily shift a model's baseline stance while having limited effect on the stance shift caused by user opinion or identity. Ultimately, our results suggest that LLM political stance is interactively and steerably vulnerable rather than being a fixed trait, highlighting how personalization may amplify identity- or opinion-conditioned shifts in the model's behaviors.

</details>


### [33] [Benevolent Bias in Multi-Turn Human-Agent Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29206)
*Qianqi Liu,Jin Huang,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"仁慈偏见"（benevolent bias）概念，通过语气与对待方式两个维度将其操作化为三类（中性支持、显性偏见、仁慈偏见），构建了包含362,880段多轮支持对话的类平衡语料库BENEVDIAL，并评测了现成安全检测器与LLM裁判两类检测器，发现现成检测器漏检仁慈偏见、LLM裁判误报上升，指出公平监测需关注对待方式的差异而不仅是表面线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中的偏见不仅表现为敌意语言，还可能以"仁慈偏见"的形式存在——不平等对待隐藏在温暖积极的语言外壳之下，现有的检测手段难以识别这种隐蔽的不公平，因此需要将其概念化、操作化并构建可检测的评测基准。

Method: 1）从语气（tone）和对待（treatment）两个维度操作化仁慈偏见，划分三类：中性支持、显性偏见、仁慈偏见；2）构建BENEVDIAL语料库，包含362,880段多轮支持对话，覆盖用户与智能体的各种人口统计属性、角色和生成器，并保持类别均衡；3）评测两类检测器：现成安全检测器与提示式LLM裁判。

Result: 现成安全检测器能可靠识别显性偏见但对仁慈偏见基本漏检；LLM裁判在更明确的检测标准下能捕捉更多仁慈偏见，但对中性支持越来越容易误判为仁慈偏见，且人口统计背景信息会放大这种误报。

Conclusion: 人机对话的公平监测不能仅依赖表面语言线索，必须深入到智能体对待用户的方式是否存在差异性（disparate treatment），强调检测范式需要超越表面信号。

Abstract: Bias in human-agent interaction can manifest not only through hostile language but also as benevolent bias, whereby unequal treatment hides behind a warm, positive tone. To make it detectable, we operationalise benevolent bias along two dimensions, tone and treatment, yielding three classes: neutral support, overt bias, and benevolent bias. Building on these definitions, we construct BENEVDIAL, a class-balanced corpus of 362,880 multi-turn support dialogues spanning user and agent demographics, roles, and generators, to support controlled evaluation. We then test two detector families on it: off-the-shelf safety detectors and prompted large language model (LLM) judges. Our results reveal a detection gap: off-the-shelf detectors reliably flag overt bias yet largely miss benevolent bias, while LLM judges catch more under more explicit detection criteria but increasingly misclassify neutral support as benevolent bias, and demographic context amplifies the false alarms. These findings suggest that fair monitoring of human-agent dialogue must look beyond surface cues to whether the agent's treatment is disparate.

</details>


### [34] [Localizing Emergent Failures in Agentic AI: Recovering Minimal Repair Families via Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29228)
*Bingjie Li,Yumeng Song,Zhongming Yao,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出最小修复族恢复（MRFR）问题及图约束联合重放（GCJR）方法，用于识别多智能体LLM系统中导致任务失败的联合必要事件集合。GCJR在受控基准和真实LLM试点上均达到1.000的家族精确匹配，同时显著减少重放调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体agentic AI系统中的失败可能源于多个LLM代理之间消息交互的联合效应。逐点归因（pointwise attribution）无法区分"联合必要修复"与"替代性单点修复"，即当多个事件共同导致失败、且单独修复其中任何一个都无法恢复任务成功时，现有方法无法识别这种需要同时修复的事件组合。

Method: 提出MRFR形式化问题定义（恢复所有在声明大小界内的包含-最小事件集，其反事实重放能恢复任务成功），并设计GCJR算法：从执行依赖图中切片与失败相关的事件，构造图可行的单点和配对候选，通过与配对干净对应物的重放进行验证。GCJR在固定重放结果下，在其声明的图域内是精确的。

Result: 在120个DAG受控基准的90个范围内案例中，GCJR实现1.000家族精确匹配，平均重放调用从56.3降至25.3（减少55.1%）。在24个案例的四智能体LLM试点中同样达到1.000家族精确匹配，平均模型调用从21.0降至10.0（减少52.4%）；单事件重放会遗漏联合必要修复。

Conclusion: 单事件级归因/重放不足以识别多智能体系统中的联合必要修复，GCJR通过图约束的联合候选构造与验证，在保证精度（家族精确匹配）的同时大幅降低计算开销，验证了其在多LLM代理系统失败诊断中的有效性。

Abstract: Failures in agentic AI systems can arise from interactions among messages exchanged by multiple large language model (LLM) agents. Pointwise attribution cannot distinguish a jointly necessary repair from alternative singleton repairs. We formulate Minimal Repair Family Recovery (MRFR): recovering all inclusion-minimal event sets whose counterfactual replay restores task success within a declared size bound. We propose Graph-Constrained Joint Replay (GCJR), which slices failure-relevant events from an execution dependency graph, constructs graph-feasible singleton and pair candidates, and verifies them by replay with paired clean counterparts. For fixed replay outcomes, GCJR is exact within its declared graph domain. On 90 in-scope cases from a 120-DAG controlled benchmark, GCJR achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match while reducing mean replay calls from 56.3 to 25.3 (55.1%) relative to exhaustive search. On a 24-case, four-agent LLM pilot, it again achieves 1.000 Family Exact Match and reduces mean model calls from 21.0 to 10.0 (52.4%); single-event replay misses jointly necessary repairs.

</details>
