*Zhiyu Chen,Keyu Zhao,Jigao Fu,Dong Liang,Yanbiao Wu,Jiaoyang Li,Haidong Xue,Xinhua Zeng,Yuanyi Zhen,Fengli Xu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ideation Arena，一个通过成对人类评估来评价LLM生成研究想法的战斗式平台。作者对14个前沿LLM和5种研究智能体架构（基于2个基础模型）进行评估，在共享封闭上下文中收集了105位计算机科学研究者的6000多次双盲成对比较，构建了Elo排名榜，并进一步构建了Ideation Arena Eval基准来检验自动评估器与人类偏好的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的研究想法难以评估，因为其科学价值无法完全由客观标准确定，也不存在单一参考答案能界定何为好想法。现有评估方法缺乏基于人类专业偏好的系统性对比。

Method: 构建Ideation Arena战斗式评估平台：1）对14个前沿LLM和5种基于2个基础模型的研究智能体架构进行成对评估；2）为所有模型提供共享的文献上下文以确保公平起点；3）从105位活跃计算机科学研究者处收集6000多次双盲成对比较；4）基于Elo评分构建提案阶段专家偏好的排行榜；5）通过评分者间一致性和鲁棒性分析验证排名稳定性；6）构建Ideation Arena Eval基准，用当前LLM作为法官测试其与人类偏好的一致性。

Result: 排行榜在不同标注者组成和领域覆盖下保持稳定。智能体有效性存在显著差异，部分框架能提升其基础模型的想法质量，而另一些几乎没有增益甚至不如基础模型。当前LLM法官仍无法可靠复现专家偏好，最佳法官在总体质量上的Soft Accuracy仅达到72.56%。

Conclusion: Ideation Arena提供了一种基于人类专家双盲成对评估的可靠研究想法评价方法，揭示了不同LLM和智能体架构在科研设想生成上的真实能力差异，并表明当前自动化评估协议尚不能完全取代人类专家判断。

Abstract: Evaluating research ideas generated by LLMs is difficult because their scientific value cannot be fully determined by objective criteria, and no single reference answer specifies what counts as a good idea. To address this challenge, we introduce Ideation Arena, a battle style platform that evaluates research ideas through pairwise human assessment. Ideation Arena evaluates ideas generated by 14 frontier LLMs and 5 research agent architectures built on 2 base models. To ensure a common starting point, Ideation Arena builds shared literature contexts from papers familiar to the participating researchers and provides the same contexts to all LLMs and agents. We collect over 6,000 double blind pairwise comparisons from 105 active computer science researchers and construct an Elo rating leaderboard of proposal-stage expert preferences in computer science under a shared closed-context protocol. We validate the rankings through interrater agreement and robustness analyses, showing that the leaderboard remains stable under changes in annotator composition and domain coverage. Our results show substantial variation in agent effectiveness, with some frameworks improving ideation quality over their backbones and others offering little benefit or even underperforming their base models. We further construct Ideation Arena Eval, a benchmark for assessing whether automated evaluators align with human preferences in research ideation. Experiments with current LLM judges show that they still cannot reliably reproduce expert preferences, with the best judge reaching 72.56% Soft Accuracy on Overall Quality. Our code, data, and leaderboards are available at https://github.com/foss12138/Research-Ideation-Arena.

</details>


### [46] [FRAMEWORKERS: A Dynamic Multi-Agent Framework for AI-Generated Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29814)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Letian Shi,Deheng Zhang,Ruibo Ming,Mengshun Hu,Dannong Xu,Jian Wang,Danda Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FRAMEWORKERS——一个以任务为中心、以工作区为基础的开放视频制作多智能体框架。中央Director通过动态编辑任务栈（Task Stack）决定下一步子任务和调用哪个子智能体，Assistant作为执行层在工作区中检索资源、调用子智能体并持久化成果。通过SFT+GRPO微调Director以提升指令路由可靠性，实验证明其在路由精度、故障恢复、跨子智能体泛化和端到端视频质量上均优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频生成模型擅长生成单条片段，但完整的视频制作需要协调长序列的相互依赖创作步骤（脚本、故事板、生成、编辑），并需要持续的资源管理和动态任务编排。现有系统要么依赖难以适应多样输入和动态工作流的刚性流水线，要么通用LLM在长程编排和多模态资源路由上不够可靠。

Method: FRAMEWORKERS由两个核心组件构成：1) Director（导演），将视频创作视为动态任务管理，持续编辑一个Task Stack来决定下一步执行哪个子任务以及调用哪个子智能体；2) Assistant（助手），作为执行层，在共享Workspace中将所选任务落地，检索所需资产和上下文，调用指定的子智能体，并持久化产生的工件。执行能力通过带注册描述符的模块化子智能体暴露，新子智能体无需重构编排流程即可集成。Director通过SFT（监督微调）加GRPO（组相对策略优化）进行微调，用于描述符条件下的任务路由。

Result: 实验表明FRAMEWORKERS在多个方面优于强LLM规划器：更高的路由准确性、对运行时故障的可靠恢复、无需重训练即可泛化到未见过的子智能体，以及相比固定流水线、单智能体系统和先前多智能体方法，实现更高的端到端视频质量和更广的任务覆盖。

Conclusion: 任务中心的动态编排结合工作区根基的智能体框架是开放视频制作的有效范式，通过模块化子智能体与强化学习的结合，可同时兼顾可扩展性、可靠性和端到端质量。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at synthesizing individual clips, but complete video production requires coordinating a long sequence of interdependent creative steps, including scripting, storyboarding, generation, and editing. It further demands persistent asset management and dynamic task orchestration as intermediate outputs, dependencies, and execution states evolve over time. Existing automated systems typically rely on rigid pipelines that are difficult to adapt to diverse inputs and changing workflows, while general-purpose large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for long-horizon orchestration and multimodal asset routing. We introduce FRAMEWORKERS, a task-centric and workspace-grounded multi-agent framework for open-ended video production. A central Director formulates video creation as dynamic task management, continuously editing a Task Stack to determine which subtask to execute next and which sub-agent to invoke. An Assistant serves as the execution layer, grounding each selected task in a shared Workspace, retrieving the required assets and context, invoking the assigned sub-agent, and persisting the resulting artifacts. Execution capabilities are exposed through modular sub-agents with registered descriptors, allowing new sub-agents to be integrated without redesigning the orchestration workflow. To improve orchestration reliability, we fine-tune the Director via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for descriptor-conditioned task routing. Experiments show that FRAMEWORKERS outperforms strong LLM planners in routing accuracy, recovers reliably from runtime failures, generalizes to unseen sub-agents without retraining, and achieves higher end-to-end video quality and broader task coverage than fixed pipelines, single-agent systems, and prior multi-agent approaches.

</details>


### [47] [Review Before Trust: Source-Grounded Integrity Gates for AI-Assisted Personal Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29965)
*Nora Girda,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"证据门控信任提升模型"，用于纵向健康记录中防止大语言模型生成的医学数据在未经源文档验证的情况下进入系统。通过确定性监控器验证引用证据、字段同行性和来源可追溯性，确保生成的数据只有在获得充分支持后才能被下游使用。实验证明该模型在技术上可行，所有测试通过，但严格策略会将部分候选人保留供人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可以将医学文档转换为结构化数据，但其输出可能看似合理却缺乏源文档支持。在纵向健康记录中持久化这些未经验证的数据会造成完整性风险：未验证的数据可能影响后续摘要、趋势或预防性护理计算。

Method: 提出证据门控信任提升模型：(1) 生成的数据保持临时状态，直到确定性监控器对照源文档验证；(2) 监控器仅在接受候选数据时验证三个条件——源中存在唯一的支持性引用、相关字段位于同一实验室行内、所需的来源可追溯性得到保留；(3) 生成器不能批准自己的输出，缺失或模糊的证据导致拒绝，被拒绝的候选数据保留供人工审查而非静默丢弃；(4) 在Medical DataCloud个人健康记录应用中实现，通过自动化测试和历史提取输出的回放进行评估。

Result: 所有22项符合性和变异测试均通过。回放覆盖9份历史实验室PDF报告，包含102个手动标记的行，产生97个数值候选：模式验证接受全部97个，早期数据包级证据检查接受94个，而强化的引用和行级策略仅接受72个并保留25个供人工审查。

Conclusion: 本研究评估的是系统完整性而非临床正确性或临床安全性。结果表明，在纵向健康记录中建立可执行的边界以防止生成的主张自行授权其重用，在技术上是可行的。

Abstract: Large language models can convert medical documents into structured data, but plausible output may still be unsupported by the source. Persisting such output in a longitudinal health record, a record that accumulates patient information over time, therefore creates an integrity risk: unverified data may influence later summaries, trends, or preventive-care computations. We introduce an evidence-gated trust-promotion model that keeps generated data provisional until a deterministic monitor verifies it against the source document. The monitor admits a candidate for a specified downstream use only when the source contains a unique supporting quotation, the relevant fields occur within the same laboratory row, and the required provenance is preserved. The generator cannot approve its own output, missing or ambiguous evidence causes refusal, and refused candidates remain available for human review rather than being silently discarded. We implement the model in Medical DataCloud, a personal health-record application, and evaluate it through automated tests and a replay of saved extraction outputs. All 22 conformance and mutation tests pass. The replay covers nine historical laboratory PDF reports containing 102 manually labelled rows. The reports produce 97 numeric candidates: schema validation accepts all 97, an earlier packet-level evidence check accepts 94, and the hardened quotation- and row-level policy admits 72 while retaining 25 for review. The study evaluates system integrity rather than clinical correctness or clinical safety. The results demonstrate the technical feasibility of an enforceable boundary that prevents generated claims from authorizing their own reuse in a longitudinal health record.

</details>


### [48] [AutoCRAT: Within-trajectory Joint Control of Stochasticity and Compute for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29988)
*Hanjun Luo,Qiushi Liu,Jingya Zhang,Haihong Pang,Jiaheng Wen,Yifei Ma,Yu Yao,Chengxi Zhang,Hanrong Zhang,Yankai Chen,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoCRAT，一个解码器侧控制器，能够在单个推理轨迹内联合调整采样随机性和推理预算，实现对冻结大语言模型的细粒度自适应推理控制。实验表明其在6个基准上减少13.8-52.7%推理token，同时提升1.5-4.5%相对准确率，并具备跨骨干网络的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的推理性能高度依赖推理时的决策，但现有策略通常是静态的、一刀切的。近期自适应方法仅单独调整解码随机性（如何探索）或推理计算量（推理多长），忽略了二者在同一推理轨迹内的交互作用。

Method: AutoCRAT采用轨迹内联合控制的视角，作为冻结骨干网络的解码器侧控制器。它仅利用解码过程中可得的信号，在生成期间联合调整采样随机性和推理预算。控制器在离散动作空间上运作，仅在语义边界处更新控制决策，从而在保持稳定性的同时对推理过程的演进保持响应。

Result: 在6个基准上的综合评估表明：AutoCRAT平均比推荐的静态配置节省13.8-52.7%的推理token；相对准确率超过静态和自适应基线1.5-4.5%；并展现出强大的跨骨干网络可迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过在轨迹内联合控制解码随机性与推理预算，AutoCRAT实现了更优的推理性价比，验证了联合控制优于孤立自适应的方法，且其仅依赖解码时可得信号的设计使其具备良好的通用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong reasoning performance, which depends critically on inference-time decisions. Yet these decisions are commonly handled by static, one-size-fits-all policies, limiting adaptation to diverse tasks and reasoning stages. Recent adaptive methods partially address this limitation, but they primarily adapt either decoding stochasticity (how the model explores) or reasoning compute (how long the model reasons) in isolation, leaving their interaction within a single reasoning trajectory unmodeled. To address this challenge, we shift toward a within-trajectory joint control view, and instantiate it in AutoCRAT, a decoder-side controller for frozen backbones. Using only signals available during decoding, AutoCRAT jointly adjusts sampling stochasticity and reasoning budget during generation. AutoCRAT operates over a discrete action space and updates control decisions only at semantic boundaries, improving stability while remaining responsive to the evolving reasoning process. Comprehensive evaluation across 6 benchmarks demonstrates that AutoCRAT (I) uses 13.8-52.7% fewer inference tokens on average than recommended static configurations, (II) surpasses recommended static and adaptive baselines by 1.5-4.5% in relative accuracy, and (III) enjoys strong cross-backbone transferability.

</details>


### [49] [Automatic Conversion of NICE Guidelines to an Executable Computational Model Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30022)
*Ashvin Gupta,Denys Prociuk,Alessandra Russo,Brendan C. Delaney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种端到端的端到端方法，利用大语言模型将NICE临床指南（自然语言文本）自动转换为可执行的、可解释的模型，能够生成针对患者的个性化建议。该方法在胰腺癌和肺癌指南上进行了验证，专家评审显示规则对齐良好，可执行模型在20个患者案例上取得了82.5%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NICE指南虽提供循证医学建议但多为非结构化自然语言，现有将其转化为可计算表示的方法通常只针对单一疾病、需要大量人工编码且不具备可扩展性。大语言模型有望自动化完成这一翻译过程，从而提升指南可计算化转换的效率和规模。

Method: 提出一种端到端方法，通过基于LLM的分步转换并辅以上下文示例，生成人类可检查的中间产物；将NICE胰腺癌和肺癌指南转换为可执行模型；通过专家评审评估规则对齐程度，并在20个患者案例上评估可执行胰腺癌模型的性能。

Result: 专家评审显示源指南与生成的可执行模型之间具有强对齐性，大多数差异是部分遗漏而非逻辑错误，幻觉或根本性错误的规则较少。在患者案例上，可执行模型取得了82.5%的F1分数。

Conclusion: LLM能够将自然语言的NICE指南转化为可解释、可执行的模型，保留指南结构，支持透明的检查和修改，并生成针对患者的个性化建议，证明了可扩展自动化生成可计算临床指南的可行性。

Abstract: Introduction: NICE guidelines provide evidence-based recommendations for clinical care but remain largely in unstructured natural language. Existing approaches to converting them into computable representations often focus on individual diseases, require substantial manual encoding, and do not scale. Large language models (LLMs) may enable much of this translation to be automated. Methods: We present an end-to-end approach that converts textual clinical guidelines into executable models capable of generating explainable, patient-specific recommendations. A stepwise LLM-based transformation with in-context examples produces human-inspectable intermediate artifacts. We apply the approach to NICE pancreatic and lung cancer guidelines, use expert review to assess rule alignment, and evaluate the executable pancreatic cancer model on 20 patient vignettes. Results: Expert review showed strong alignment between the source guidelines and generated executable models. Most discrepancies were partial omissions rather than incorrect logic, while hallucinated or fundamentally incorrect rules were rare. On the patient vignettes, the executable model achieved an F1 score of 82.5%. Conclusion: LLMs can transform natural-language NICE guidelines into interpretable, executable models that preserve guideline structure, support transparent inspection and modification, and generate patient-specific recommendations. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of scalable automated generation of computable clinical guidelines.

</details>


### [50] [Can LLM Agents Discover? Evaluating Creativity on ML Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30047)
*Shitanshu Bhushan,Yunxiang Zhang,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估多轮LLM研究型Agent创造力的框架，从P-Creativity（心理新颖性）、H-Creativity（历史新颖性）和Usefulness（任务性能）三个维度出发，在10个Kaggle风格的ML任务上对AIDE和AIRA-Dojo两个Agent框架进行评估。研究发现所有Agent在从探索转向利用时P-Creativity下降，LLM的H-Creativity高于获奖人类但任务性能更低，表明当前Agent能探索解空间的新颖区域但无法将新颖性转化为性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管当前的AI系统声称能够实现自主科学发现并产出研究论文，但对其是否真正具有创造力（同时具备新颖性和实用性）的评估仍然是一个开放问题，缺乏系统性的创造力评估框架。

Method: 以ML工程任务为测试平台，构建三维创造力评估框架（P-Creativity、H-Creativity、Usefulness）；开发了LLM-as-a-Judge流水线用于P-Creativity的大规模自动化评估，并通过与人类创造力评判的相关性验证其可靠性；在来自MLE-Bench的10个Kaggle式ML任务上评估AIDE和AIRA-Dojo两个Agent框架的轨迹。

Result: (1) 所有Agent在从探索阶段过渡到利用阶段时均表现出P-Creativity下降；(2) LLM的H-Creativity高于获奖人类，但其任务性能更低；LLM-as-a-Judge流水线与人类创造力评判具有强相关性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM研究Agent能够在解空间中探索新颖区域，但缺乏将这些新颖性转化为实际任务性能改进的能力，这揭示了创造力中新奇性与实用性之间的关键差距。

Abstract: Recent AI systems promise autonomous scientific discovery, claiming to discover algorithms and produce research papers, yet understanding whether they exhibit creativity, the capacity to produce solutions that are both novel and useful, remains an open question. We present a framework for evaluating multi-turn LLM research agents' creativity using ML engineering tasks as a testbed, through three dimensions: P-Creativity (psychological novelty: novel relative to the agent's own prior solutions within a run), H-Creativity (historical novelty: novel relative to the corpus of human solutions), and Usefulness (task performance). Evaluating two agent frameworks, AIDE and AIRA-Dojo, on 10 Kaggle-style machine learning tasks from MLE-Bench, we develop an LLM-as-a-Judge pipeline and verify its strong correlation with human creativity judgments, providing a reliable automated metric for P-Creativity evaluation at scale. Applying this pipeline to agent trajectories, we find: (1) all agents exhibit declining P-Creativity as they transition from exploration to exploitation; (2) LLMs exhibit greater H-Creativity than medal-winning humans, yet achieve lower performance. Our findings reveal that current agents can explore novel regions of the solution space but lack the capacity to convert this novelty into improved task performance.

</details>


### [51] [Spec2Twin-Chain: Orchestrating Bi-Level Optimization with LLMs for Blockchain Digital Twin Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30050)
*Haoting Zhang,Haoxian Chen,Jiayuan Sheng,Donglin Zhan,Zeyu Zheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spec2Twin-Chain，一个将区块链数字孪生构建形式化为双层优化问题的框架：上层由大语言模型根据系统规格和行为证据提议并修订架构，下层由基于仿真的优化器校准参数，两层级联迭代，并将评估结果存入全局档案以指导后续提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建区块链数字孪生需要将领域知识和系统描述转化为仿真架构、校准参数并验证，但这些步骤通常依赖于特定应用的建模工作，难以在不同系统和下游决策问题中复用，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 将数字孪生构建建模为双层优化问题。上层使用大语言模型基于系统规格、行为证据和已评估设计的反馈来提出并修订结构上可行的架构；下层使用基于仿真的优化器在显式目标和护栏约束下校准条件于架构的参数。两层迭代进行，下层评估的候选对象保留在全局档案中，用于指导上层的后续提议。

Result: 实验覆盖孪生校准、反馈驱动恢复、压力分析、下游策略优化和策略更新。结果表明框架能够构建行为准确的孪生模型，通过迭代反馈改进初始设计，并复用已校准的孪生模型支持下游决策。

Conclusion: Spec2Twin-Chain有效实现了区块链数字孪生构建的自动化，在行为保真度和跨系统复用方面均表现良好，支持下游决策任务的端到端应用。

Abstract: Building a blockchain digital twin largely requires translating domain knowledge and specific system descriptions into a simulator architecture, calibrating its parameters against behavioral evidence, and validating the constructed twin. These steps are commonly performed through application-specific modeling efforts that can be difficult to reuse across systems and downstream decision problems. We consider automating this process through Spec2Twin-Chain, a framework that formulates blockchain digital-twin construction as a bi-level optimization problem. At the upper level, a large language model proposes and revises structurally admissible architectures using system specifications, behavioral evidence, and feedback from evaluated designs. At the lower level, a simulation-based optimizer calibrates the architecture-conditioned parameters under explicit objectives and guardrail constraints. The two levels iterate. The evaluated candidates at lower levels are retained in a global archive and used to guide subsequent proposals at upper levels. We conduct controlled experiments involving twin calibration, feedback-driven recovery, stress analysis, downstream policy optimization, and policy updating. The results demonstrate that the framework can construct behaviorally accurate twins, improve initial designs through iterative feedback, and reuse calibrated twins to support downstream decisions.

</details>


### [52] [LLM-Based Knowledge Graph Completion Combining Discrete Structural Coding with Similar Entity Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30235)
*Jiaqi Wang,Dongying Lin,Yang Yang,Yinan Liu,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoSC方法，结合离散结构编码与相似实体信息，用于基于LLM的知识图谱补全。LLM先从离散结构码生成候选实体排序，再利用与查询实体结构相似的实体信息进行细化，在FB15k-237上MRR和Hits@10超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱补全需要模型同时利用文本描述和关系结构。现有基于LLM的方法要么将KG结构编码为离散token，要么只对受限的候选实体集合进行精炼，这两条路线很大程度上被分开研究，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出CoSC（Combining discrete Structure coding with Similar entity information）。具体流程：先用LLM基于离散结构编码生成初始候选实体排序，然后收集与查询实体结构相似的实体的信息，用这些信息对候选排序进行细化。

Result: 在FB15k-237数据集上，CoSC在MRR和Hits@10指标上超越了现有基线，在Hits@1上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将离散结构编码与相似实体信息相结合能够有效提升基于LLM的知识图谱补全性能，尤其在排序指标上表现出明显优势。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion requires models to use both textual descriptions and relational structure. Existing LLM-based methods either encode KG structure as discrete tokens or refine a restricted set of candidate entities, and these two directions have largely been studied separately. We propose CoSC for LLM-based KGC, which combines discrete structural coding with similar entity information. Specifically, an LLM generates an initial candidate entity ranking from discrete structural codes, after which information from entities with structures similar to that of the query entity refines the ranking. Experiments on FB15k-237 show that CoSC outperforms existing baselines on MRR and Hits@10 while remaining competitive on Hits@1.

</details>


### [53] [Generating Workflow DAGs from Natural Language with Non-Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30250)
*Anand Iyer,Bhanu Khetharpal,Srinivas Upadhya,Ramkumar Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号分解方法，将业务管理员编写的自然语言路由规则翻译为可执行的企业联络中心工作流DAG图。方法将组合图构建从大语言模型转移到确定性编译器，由学习式注册表选择前端聚焦生成，从而让低成本非推理型LLM达到接近生产级质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业联络中心中，业务管理员编写的自然语言路由规则需要转换为可执行的工作流图（DAG，含条件动作、并行分支、hit-first回退链和分支级布尔谓词）。使用昂贵的扩展推理模型成本过高，而低成本非推理模型在生成复杂DAG时存在"发射密度瓶颈"——即随着单次发射中相互依赖节点数量的增加，节点选择准确但属性配置和布尔分组错误率上升。

Method: 将组合图构建从模型中剥离，放入由紧凑中间表示驱动的确定性编译器；同时设计了一个学习式注册表选择前端，聚焦生成于相关词表。模型只需生成紧凑中间表示，编译器负责最终DAG的构造与约束满足。

Result: 在635条规则的合成基准上，跨四个模型，系统达到约89%的LLM裁判有效性、约90%的精确匹配条件准确率、99-100%的有效JSON，且每条规则使用的提示词token约为单体提示词的一半。在GPT-5.3-chat上，该方法将裁判有效性提升24个百分点，并与推理模型的开箱即用质量在统计上等效，不过仍存在约8个百分点的前沿差距。

Conclusion: 神经符号分解是结构化生成任务中的有效策略：通过将组合学工作从模型转移到确定性编译器，可以显著降低对昂贵推理模型的依赖而保持生产级质量。论文还提供了部署路径和可迁移的经验教训。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of translating natural-language routing rules written by business administrators into executable workflow graphs for enterprise contact centers. Each target is a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of conditional actions with parallel branches, hit-first fallback chains, and per-branch Boolean predicates, encoded in the JSON dialect of a commercial routing platform. We show that neuro-symbolic decomposition enables lower-cost, non-reasoning large language models to generate complex workflow DAGs at production-relevant quality without expensive extended-reasoning models. Our central diagnostic is an emission-density bottleneck: on a 635-rule benchmark of manufactured synthetic data, models select the correct graph nodes with high accuracy but increasingly misconfigure attributes and Boolean grouping as the number of interdependent nodes emitted in one pass grows. We therefore move combinatorial graph construction from the model into a deterministic compiler driven by a compact intermediate representation, with a learned registry-selection front end that focuses generation on relevant vocabulary. Across four models, the full system reaches approximately 89% LLM-judge validity, approximately 90% exact-match condition accuracy, and 99-100% valid JSON while using roughly half the per-rule prompt tokens of a monolithic prompt. On GPT-5.3-chat, the method improves judge validity by 24 percentage points and achieves statistical equivalence to a reasoning model's out-of-the-box quality, although an approximately 8-point frontier gap remains. We also present a deployment path and transferable lessons for structured-generation applications.

</details>


### [54] [Ignorance or Incompetence? Constructing Knowledge-Gated, Verifiable Tasks for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30322)
*Hanlin Tian,Minhao Li,Yu Mi,Sihan Zhu,Zhao Yang,Yuxiang Wang,Hongquan Zhu,Qiufei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识门控的任务构建协议，通过将任务指令与包含私有约定、参考表和实用算子的"知识工件"分离，使基准测试能够显式、可测试地控制智能体是否访问这些专业约定。实验表明，一个前沿智能体配置在提供工件时通过率达68.0%，不提供时为0%，验证了该协议能可靠地分离"知识可获得性"这一变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业智能体任务往往依赖公开语料中不存在的行业约定，但现有基准测试很少控制智能体是否真正获得了这些约定。这导致难以区分模型失败是因为缺乏领域知识还是缺乏推理能力，也无法对"知识注入"的效果进行可靠评估。

Method: 提出知识门控任务构建协议：将任务指令与紧凑的"工件"（包含私有约定、参考表和实用算子）分离；通过构建期溯源、跨条件字节级相同的任务指令、泄漏审计和可执行见证确保对工件依赖的显式性和可测试性。采用确定性求解器和规则语料为结构化任务提供精确真值，使用命名标准级评分标准（rubrics）支持无法由单一可执行预言机检查的输出。通过配置相对校准筛选保留通过五试实验验证的任务。

Result: 在15个校准任务中，一个前沿智能体配置在提供工件时通过率达68.0%，不提供时为0%；在某一任务上，使用看似合理但错误的人工制品，五次试验也全部失败（0%）。校准筛选最终保留7个通过五试经验知识门控筛选的任务。部分任务套件和工具已公开。

Conclusion: 实验验证了构建协议的行为有效性，但作者明确指出这些结果并不证明保留的任务能改善训练后（post-training）效果。协议本身是一个可用的、可审计的任务构建框架，但对下游训练价值的验证仍需进一步工作。

Abstract: Professional agent tasks often depend on conventions that are absent from public corpora, yet benchmarks rarely control whether an agent has access to those conventions. We introduce a knowledge-gated task-construction protocol that separates a task instruction from a compact artefact containing private conventions, reference tables, and utility operators. Construction-time provenance, byte-identical task instructions across the provided- and withheld-artefact conditions, leak audits, and executable witnesses make dependence on the artefact explicit and testable. Across fifteen calibration tasks, one frontier agent configuration achieves a 68.0% pass rate with the artefact and 0% without it; on one task, a plausible but incorrect artefact also yields 0% across five trials. Deterministic solvers and rule corpora provide exact ground truth for structured tasks, while named criterion-level rubrics support outputs that cannot be checked by a single executable oracle. A configuration-relative calibration screen retains seven tasks satisfying our five-trial empirical knowledge-gating screen. These experiments validate the behavior of the construction protocol; they do not establish that the retained tasks improve post-training. We publicly release part of the task suite and supporting tooling at https://github.com/DatagridsAI/Knowledge-Gated-Task-Construction.

</details>


### [55] [Answer Probing-Guided Search for Diverse Solution Exploration of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30345)
*Yi Fang,Que Shen,Chengpeng Li,Boyi Deng,Wei Shi,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Fengli Xu,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Answer Probing方法，通过探测LLM从中间推理路径到达的可能答案，利用探测答案的隐藏状态相似性和困惑度来引导树搜索（APTS），从而生成更多样化的高质量解决方案，在三个推理任务和两个LLM上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在推理时倾向于收敛到单一高置信度解决方案，限制了替代有效解路径的探索。现有的测试时方法通过响应级语义嵌入来剪枝语义相似的树枝，但这些嵌入容易被语言和风格上的相似性混淆，难以区分真正不同的解路径。

Method: 提出Answer Probing方法，从中间推理路径探测LLM可能到达的答案；利用探测答案的隐藏状态来更好地区分不同的解路径，并使用探测答案的困惑度作为推理正确性的实际代理指标。基于此，提出Answer Probing-Guided Tree Search (APTS)，使用探测答案的隐藏状态相似性和困惑度来引导树搜索过程。

Result: 在三个推理任务和两个LLM上的实验表明，APTS持续提升了解决方案的多样性，证明了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 探测答案的隐藏状态比语义嵌入更能有效区分不同解路径，且困惑度可作为推理正确性的实用代理。APTS方法能有效提升LLM生成解决方案的多样性。

Abstract: Generating multiple diverse and high-quality solutions is valuable for many applications, such as code-test generation and drug discovery. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to converge on a single high-confidence solution during inference, limiting exploration of alternative valid solution paths. Existing test-time methods promote diversity through tree-like search and prune semantically similar branches using response-level semantic embeddings. However, we find that such embeddings are easily confounded by linguistic and stylistic similarities, making it difficult to distinguish genuinely distinct solution paths. To address this, we introduce Answer Probing, which probes the potential answer an LLM would reach from an intermediate reasoning path. We demonstrate that the hidden states of probed answers more effectively differentiate distinct solution paths than semantic embeddings, and the perplexity of probed answers serves as a practical proxy for reasoning correctness. Based on these findings, we propose Answer Probing-Guided Tree Search (APTS), which guides the tree search by the probed answers' hidden state similarity and perplexity. Experiments on three reasoning tasks across two LLMs show that APTS consistently enhances solution diversity, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [56] [Will the User Ever Know? Covert Indirect Prompt Injection on Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30362)
*Yunseok Lee,Yunji Kim,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了对间接提示注入攻击更细粒度的评估指标——将ASR分解为隐蔽成功率（CSR）和显性成功率（OSR），并据此提出了ICoA攻击方法，通过引导智能体在执行注入后回归用户任务来实现隐蔽成功，在AgentDojo基准上相对于最强基线提升了3.79-12.01个百分点的CSR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ASR指标仅统计注入是否成功，忽略了用户是否能在最终响应中察觉注入痕迹。作者通过分析成功注入轨迹发现存在两种截然不同的结果：隐蔽成功（智能体执行注入但返回正常响应）和显性成功（智能体在最终响应中报告注入行为）。准确区分这两类成功对于评估IPI的真实威胁至关重要。

Method: 作者首先将ASR分解为CSR（最终响应无注入痕迹的成功）和OSR（用户可察觉的成功）。通过分析ReAct格式下成功轨迹，发现隐蔽与显性成功的分界取决于注入后智能体的行为：隐蔽轨迹在攻击执行后将控制权交还给用户任务，而显性轨迹以攻击本身结束；这是因为ReAct的最终响应只总结最近一次动作。基于此，作者提出ICoA（Induced Covert Attack）攻击方法，通过在执行注入后引导智能体返回用户任务来诱导隐蔽结局。

Result: 在AgentDojo基准上的四个目标模型中，ICoA实现了最高的CSR，相对于最强基线在CSR上获得了3.79至12.01个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提出了更细致的评估指标来揭示IPI攻击的真实威胁程度，还验证了攻击者可以通过操纵智能体在注入后的行为（即回归用户任务）来实现更隐蔽的攻击，为理解和防御间接提示注入攻击提供了新的视角。

Abstract: As LLM agents take real-world actions through tools, indirect prompt injection (IPI) has emerged as a serious threat. The standard metric, Attack Success Rate (ASR), counts whether an injection succeeds but ignores what the user notices in the agent's final response. Looking at successful injection traces, we find two distinct outcomes: the agent executes the injection while returning an otherwise normal response, or reports the injected action in its final response, giving the user a chance to notice. We call these covert and overt successes. From the user's perspective, we decompose ASR into the Covert Success Rate (CSR), counting successes leaving no trace in the final response, and the Overt Success Rate (OSR), counting successes the user can detect. To understand what drives the gap, we analyze successful trajectories and find that the agent's behavior after the injection separates covert from overt: covert traces hand control back to the user task before ending, while overt traces end at the attack itself. This split follows from the ReAct format, where the final response summarizes the most recent action. Building on this observation, we propose ICoA (Induced Covert Attack), an IPI attack designed to induce covert outcomes by steering the agent back to the user task after executing the injection. Across four target models on AgentDojo, ICoA achieves the highest CSR, with gains of 3.79-12.01 percentage points over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [57] [Augmenting Human Performance with an XR Agent Learning from Online Behavior and BCI Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30369)
*Ziheng Li,Xichen He,Haoyan Chen,Charlie Zou,Sheng Bai,Benjamin Yang,Mengyuan Wu,Jake Ledner,Yi-Jie Cheng,Akito Yamauchi,Dishita G Turakhia,Steven Feiner,Paul Sajda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OLIVE框架，将被动脑电图（EEG）与行为信号在线融合，用于实时辅助高动态、高压力任务。OLIVE通过联合估计各信号源的可靠性，无需手动标签或离线训练，即可持续适配冻结的视觉-语言模型，帮助用户检测和参与超出其原始行动带宽的目标。三组用户研究证明OLIVE在收敛率和收敛速度上Pareto优于先前测试时适配框架，且融合生理与行为信号的智能体在目标无声切换时的重收敛速度比仅行为信号快1.27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高时间压力、高动态和高风险任务中，用户常受限于自身的行动带宽，难以在短时间内检测和响应所有相关目标。现有人机协作方法依赖显式行为信号或离线训练，缺乏快速、实时、无需标注的适配能力，难以在任务动态变化时提供及时可靠的辅助。

Method: OLIVE将被动EEG（固定锁定）与显式行为信号在线融合，联合估计每个信号源的可靠性，从而在不进行离线训练或人工标注的情况下，持续适配冻结的视觉-语言模型（VLM）的推理，动态判断哪些项目与当前任务相关，并为用户提供实时指导。

Result: 通过三项用户研究（包括两次XR环境中OLIVE驱动的辅助智能体的实况部署），结果显示：OLIVE在收敛率和收敛速度上Pareto优于先前的测试时适配框架；融合EEG与行为信号的智能体在目标无声切换时可显著加快重收敛速度（平均1.27倍，p=0.008），且性能提升与个体技能水平基本无关。

Conclusion: 被动EEG与行为信号的在线融合能够有效扩展用户的行动带宽，实现对动态任务中目标检测与参与能力的可靠提升。OLIVE在任务变化瞬间能提供更快的重收敛，从而在关键时刻恢复可信赖的辅助，具有在高压力人机协作场景中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: We present OLIVE, a framework for adapting a foundation model to provide real-time assistance in temporally demanding, high-stakes, and dynamic tasks. We show that passive EEG, fused online with behavioral evidence, can meaningfully extend the number of targets users detect and engage beyond their unaided action bandwidth. OLIVE learns from both explicit behavioral signals (the targets the user shoots down in an XR first-person shooter game) and implicit physiological signals (fixation-locked EEG) to provide timely guidance, continuously adapting a frozen vision-language model's inference on which items are task-relevant by jointly estimating per-source reliability without manual labels or offline training. Through three user studies, including two live deployments of an assistive agent driven by OLIVE in XR, we show that OLIVE Pareto-dominates prior test-time adaptation frameworks, achieving the highest convergence rate at comparable convergence speed. Combining implicit physiological and explicit behavioral signals, the OLIVE agent produces the largest and most reliable within-session improvement to a user's ability to detect and engage targets, largely independent of the individual's skill. When the target switches silently, the agent that uses both behavioral and physiological signals reconverges significantly faster than the behavior-only agent (1.27 times faster on average, p = .008), restoring trustworthy guidance at the moment the task changes, precisely when reliable assistance matters most.

</details>


### [58] [EvoSkill Injection: Red-Teaming Autonomous Skill Generation and Evolution in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30429)
*Doyun Kim,Chanwoo Kim,Sugyeong Eo,Yeo-Chan Yoon,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvoSkill Injection威胁模型，针对自进化智能体的自主技能生成与演化管道。作者构建了SARGE红队框架（通过迭代生成、升级和强化互动来评估该威胁），并配套构建了EvoSkillBench恶意交互轨迹数据集和EvoSkillSafetyBench攻击后基准。实验表明SARGE能诱导恶意技能的生成，且注入的技能会被持久存储并反复激活，暴露了持续性能力腐败的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体系统日益采用技能化架构以减少重复推理成本并提升任务执行的稳定性与效率。近期研究提出自进化智能体，能够自主生成、精炼和复用技能以实现持续能力演进。然而，自主技能进化引入了新的攻击面——恶意能力可能被生成、存储并作为合法技能被复用，目前缺乏对这一威胁的系统性研究与评估。

Method: 首先定义EvoSkill Injection威胁模型，针对自进化智能体的自主技能生成与演化管道。然后提出SARGE红队框架，通过迭代生成（generation）、升级（escalation）和强化（reinforcement）三种交互机制来评估该威胁。为支撑框架，构建EvoSkillBench基准数据集（包含用于诱导恶意技能形成的恶意交互轨迹）和EvoSkillSafetyBench攻击后基准（评估注入的恶意技能是否会被检索并激活为有害行为）。

Result: 评估结果表明：SARGE能够成功诱导恶意技能的形成；注入的恶意技能会被持久存储在技能库中，并在后续任务中被反复检索和激活，产生有害行为，揭示了持续性能力腐败的严重风险。

Conclusion: 自进化智能体的自主技能演化管道存在严重的安全漏洞，恶意技能注入可导致持久性的能力腐败。本文提出的威胁模型、红队框架和基准数据集为该领域的安全研究奠定了基础，强调了在技能自进化架构中需要引入安全监控与防御机制。

Abstract: LLM-based agent systems increasingly adopt skill-based architectures to reduce repetitive reasoning costs and improve stable, efficient task execution. Recent studies propose self-evolving agents that autonomously generate, refine, and reuse skills from past experiences to enable continuous capability evolution. However, autonomous skill evolution introduces a new attack surface in which malicious capabilities are generated, stored, and reused as legitimate skills. In this paper, we define EvoSkill Injection as a threat model targeting the autonomous skill generation and evolution pipeline of self-evolving agents. We further propose SARGE (Red-teaming Autonomous Skill Generation and Evolution in self-evolving agents), a red-teaming framework for evaluating this threat model through iterative generation, escalation, and reinforcement interactions. To support our framework, we construct EvoSkillBench, a benchmark dataset of malicious interaction trajectories for inducing malicious skill formation in self-evolving agents, and introduce EvoSkillSafetyBench, a post-attack benchmark for evaluating whether injected malicious skills are subsequently retrieved and activated as harmful behaviors. Our evaluation shows that SARGE induces malicious skill formation and that injected skills are persistently stored and repeatedly activated, highlighting the risk of persistent capability corruption.

</details>


### [59] [CHASE: How Content Ecosystems Are Reshaped When Ranking Is the Only Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30466)
*Qianwen Gao,Zichang Su,Yiwen Hou,Arlen Kumar,Leanid Palkhouski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHASE框架，一个用于研究LLM检索系统中内容生态在重复优化下如何被重塑的受控仿真框架。通过20轮迭代（排名、特征区分、重写、评估），发现所有六个领域中质量与排名对齐度均下降（Spearman's rho平均变化-0.068），证实重复适应LLM排名信号会导致内容同质化和质量-排名错位，且该效应源于排名激励机制而非单纯重写本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎优化（GEO）已被广泛用于提升内容在基于LLM的检索系统中的可见性，但其在重复优化下对内容群体层面的影响尚未被充分理解。已有研究多聚焦单轮优化效果，缺乏对内容生态系统动态演化的系统性分析。

Method: 提出CHASE受控仿真框架，将排名作为来源可见性的代理，并用六个领域中对引用生成响应的排名-引用AUC（0.853±0.093）验证此抽象。框架循环执行排名、特征区分、重写和评估四个步骤，迭代20轮，并设置随机目标对照组以区分排名激励适应与重写本身的效应。

Result: 所有六个领域中质量与排名对齐度均下降：从R0到R20，Spearman's rho变化范围为-0.107至-0.018，平均变化-0.068。随机目标对照实验表明该降低与对排名激励的适应相关，而非单纯迭代重写。生态系统动态表现出强烈的领域依赖性。

Conclusion: 反复对固定LLM排名信号进行优化会重塑内容种群结构及创作者面临的激励机制，导致内容同质化和质量-排名错位，提示需要关注GEO的长期生态效应。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) is increasingly used to improve content visibility in LLM-based retrieval systems, yet its population-level effects under repeated optimization remain poorly understood. We introduce Content Homogenization under rAnking Signal Exploitation (CHASE), a controlled simulation framework for studying how content ecosystems are reshaped when creators repeatedly adapt documents to an LLM ranking signal. We use ranking as a proxy for source visibility and validate this abstraction against citations in grounded generated responses, obtaining a rank-citation AUC of 0.853 $\pm$ 0.093 across six domains. CHASE then iterates ranking, feature discrimination, rewriting, and evaluation over 20 rounds across different domains. Quality-ranking alignment decreases in all six domains: from R0 to R20, the change in Spearman's rho ranges from -0.107 to -0.018, with a mean change of -0.068, which means documents closer to the ranking feature profile become less aligned with independently judged document quality over the simulation horizon. A random-target control has shown that it is associated with adaptation toward ranking-derived incentives rather than iterative rewriting alone. The resulting ecosystem dynamics are strongly domain-dependent. Together, these findings show how repeated optimization against a fixed LLM ranking signal can reshape both content populations and the incentives faced by content creators.

</details>


### [60] [ScienceArena: Benchmarking LLMs on Latest Scientific Olympiad Competitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30517)
*Guangxiang Zhao,Qilong Shi,Xusen Xiao,Wenpu Liu,Yaoming Li,Linfeng Hao,Shuyang Hou,Zijian Guo,Xinrui Zhang,Yuntian Zhao,Zhengyang Wang,Wenrui Liu,Yuhan Wu,Tong Yang,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScienceArena 是一个基于十三项公开科学竞赛（如 IPhO、IChO、IBO、USAPhO、USNCO 等）构建的奥林匹克风格基准测试，用于评估前沿大语言模型（LLM）的科研推理能力。该基准采用过程信用评分标准，通过专家审核的数字化流程将官方考试、图表、解答和评分标准转化为结构化题目，并利用奖牌得主标注的真相校准 LLM 作为评判者以扩展评估规模。实验表明，最强的评判模型能在专家总分一分以内保持一致性；对十四个最新 LLM 的评估显示，顶尖模型在多项公开国际考试中获得奖牌等效评分，但化学和长时程一致性仍是关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 基准测试存在饱和问题和数据污染，越来越难以真实反映模型在科学推理方面的能力。作者希望构建一个难以通过数据记忆取胜的、具备开放性多步骤推理和严格评分标准的科学基准。

Method: （1）从十三项公开科学竞赛中收集题目，涵盖物理、化学和生物；（2）构建专家审核的数字化流程，将官方考试、图形、解答和评分标准转为结构化项目，并由奥林匹克奖牌得主审核；（3）利用奖牌得主的真实评分校准 LLM-as-judge，以替代昂贵的人工评分；（4）使用交错求解方式评估十四个最新 LLM。

Result: 两个强评判 LLM 在专家总分一分以内保持一致；奖牌得主的笔记显示失败多源于视觉锚定、结构保真和全局问题控制而非术语缺失；顶尖模型在多项公开国际考试中获得奖牌等效分数，但化学表现和长时程一致性仍是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: ScienceArena 提供了一个难以作弊、评分严格且可扩展的科学推理基准，揭示了当前前沿 LLM 在科学竞赛推理中的实际水平与关键短板，尤其是化学推理和长时间多步骤任务中的一致性不足。

Abstract: Benchmark saturation and data contamination increasingly obscure genuine scientific reasoning in frontier LLMs. We introduce \textsc{ScienceArena}, an olympiad-style benchmark from thirteen public science competitions in physics, chemistry, and biology, including IPhO and IChO 2025--2026, IBO 2023, USAPhO 2026, and USNCO 2025. Its open-ended, multi-step problems use process-credit rubrics, making faithful scoring difficult. We build ScienceArena through an expert-audited digitization pipeline that converts official exams, figures, solutions, and rubrics into structured items verified by olympiad medalists. To scale evaluation beyond costly human grading, we calibrate LLM-as-judge against medalist ground truth on archived answers from five models across IPhO and IChO; two strong judges stay within one point of expert total scores. Medalist notes show that failures often stem from visual grounding, structure fidelity, and global problem control rather than missing terminology. Evaluating fourteen recent LLMs with interleaved solving, we find that top models obtain medal-equivalent rubric scores on several public international exams, while chemistry and long-horizon consistency remain key bottlenecks. We provide an interactive \href{https://science-arena.onrender.com/}{demo}.

</details>


### [61] [Designing an Auditable LLM-Supported Workflow for Qualitative Thematic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30543)
*Nadia Jul Jeldtoft,Tariq Yousef*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可审计且隐私保护的LLM辅助归纳性潜在主题分析（TA）计算方法，通过五项设计原则、两阶段工作流和评估框架实现，并在丹麦语访谈数据上验证了其可行性与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM为扩展定性分析提供了新可能，但现有应用在方法论透明度方面存在不足，即如何将定性方法转化为计算流程缺乏清晰的交代。本文旨在弥合这一差距，提供一种在方法论上透明、可审计且隐私保护的计算化TA操作方案。

Method: 本文首先从TA的方法论要求和LLM推理条件中推导出五项设计原则：保留解释性语境、维持经验材料与分析输出之间的可追溯关系、显式表示分析构念与推理、将LLM推理约束在解释性任务上、支持隐私保护的本地部署。其次提出了一个两阶段工作流的概念验证，将解释性LLM推理与确定性程序控制相结合，生成代码、分析论证、主题和主题描述，同时保留与源材料的显式链接。最后提出了一个评估框架，将结构比较（与人工TA对比）与独立专家评估相结合。

Result: 在丹麦语半结构化访谈转录数据上的评估表明：该工作流生成的代码级输出在覆盖率上与人工标注大致相当，分析论证获得高度评价，但生成的主题结构更为压缩，表现为更少且更宽泛的主题。

Conclusion: 研究证明了通过模块化工作流实现可审计的LLM辅助TA的可行性。该工作流可扩展至更大数据集、兼容不同LLM，并支持跨研究领域迁移，领域适配主要需要调整提示策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer new possibilities for scaling qualitative analysis, but existing applications often provide limited methodological transparency regarding how qualitative methods are translated into computational procedures. This paper presents an auditable and privacy-preserving computational operationalization of inductive and latent Thematic Analysis (TA). This paper first derives five design principles from the methodological requirements of TA and the conditions introduced by LLM-based inference: preserving interpretative context, maintaining traceable relationships between empirical material and analytical outputs, representing analytical constructs and reasoning explicitly, constraining LLM inference to interpretative tasks, and enabling privacy-preserving local deployment. Second, it presents a proof-of-concept for a two-phase workflow that operationalizes these principles by combining interpretative LLM inference with deterministic procedural control to generate codes, analytical justifications, themes, and theme descriptions while preserving explicit links to the source material. Third, it proposes an evaluation framework combining structural comparison with human-led TA and independent expert assessment of analytical quality. The evaluation is conducted on semi-structured Danish interview transcripts. and the results shows that the workflow produces code-level outputs with coverage broadly comparable to human annotations and highly rated analytical justifications, while generating a more compressed thematic structure characterized by fewer and broader themes. The findings demonstrate the feasibility of auditable LLM-supported TA through a modular workflow designed to scale to larger datasets, accommodate different LLMs, and support transfer across research domains, with domain adaptation primarily requiring adjustments to the prompting strategy.

</details>


### [62] [AdaPath: Query-Adaptive Path-Finding via Path-Bank for Multi-Hop Implicit Biomedical KGQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30556)
*Jun Hyeong Kim,Dongki Kim,Yinhua Piao,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AdaPath框架，通过Path-Bank中的查询自适应元路径来检索，应对生物医学知识图谱问答中查询缺乏中间推理信息及图谱稠密连接导致路径寻找易出错的两大挑战；同时发布BioStrat-QA基准数据集，AdaPath在多个生物医学KGQA基准上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱上的路径寻找已成为在多跳问题上支撑LLM推理的有效方式，但生物医学问答面临两个通用方法未设计解决的独特挑战：(i)查询不暴露中间推理过程且可通过多条有效路径回答；(ii)生物医学知识图谱稠密连接，路径寻找方法容易走错方向。这些挑战需要专门设计的方法。

Method: 提出AdaPath路径寻找框架，从Path-Bank中检索查询自适应的元路径，该元路径同时捕获查询语义和生物医学知识图谱结构。AdaPath在补充生物医学查询中缺失的线索的同时，在多跳推理中对稠密知识图谱邻域进行有效剪枝。此外发布了BioStrat-QA基准数据集，该基准按多跳查询暴露中间推理的程度对查询进行分层。

Result: 在多个生物医学KGQA基准上，AdaPath一致优于基线方法，即使在多跳查询暴露较少表层信息的情况下仍能维持有意义的路径寻找效果。

Conclusion: AdaPath通过查询自适应元路径机制有效解决了生物医学KGQA中的推理线索缺失和知识图谱稠密两大问题，同时发布BioStrat-QA基准供社区进一步评估，源代码已公开。

Abstract: Path-finding over knowledge graphs has become an effective way to ground LLM reasoning on multi-hop questions. However, biomedical QA introduces two distinct challenges that general-domain methods are not designed for: (i) queries do not expose intermediate reasoning and can be answered through multiple valid pathways, and (ii) biomedical knowledge graphs are densely connected, so path-finding methods easily take wrong turns. To address these challenges, we propose AdaPath, a path-finding framework that retrieves query-adaptive meta-paths from Path-Bank, which captures both query semantics and biomedical knowledge graph structure. AdaPath provides the missing cues in biomedical queries while effectively pruning dense knowledge graph neighborhoods during multi-hop reasoning. We further release BioStrat-QA, a biomedical KGQA benchmark that stratifies multi-hop queries by how much intermediate reasoning they expose. Across biomedical KGQA benchmarks, AdaPath consistently outperforms baselines, sustaining meaningful path-finding even when multi-hop queries expose less surface information. The source code is available at https://github.com/Jun-Hyeong-Kim/AdaPath.

</details>


### [63] [Automated Testing of LLM-Based Post Hoc Explainers Using Model Checking as an Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30581)
*Dennis Gross,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统测试LLM作为顺序决策策略事后解释器的方法，利用概率模型检查作为测试预言机提供精确参考结果，并通过后验查询分类法生成结构化的测试用例集，按诊断难度进行优先级排序。在七个MDP环境中，该方法有效区分了三个开源LLM的解释忠实度（推理模型85%、中型模型70%、1B模型低于随机基线），并为无预言机的无模型场景提供了可信度参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽被用作事后解释器来生成自然语言策略解释，但常产生看似合理实则错误的陈述，而现有方法缺乏系统性测试解释忠实度的途径。两个核心障碍是：缺少判断解释正确性的预言机，以及自然语言查询缺乏结构化输入空间导致难以系统生成测试用例。

Method: 采用概率模型检查作为测试预言机，精确计算参考结果用于自动评判LLM答案；构建事后查询类别的分类法，围绕策略解释所依赖的环境级事实来结构化输入空间，并基于问题特定诊断难度分数对生成的测试用例进行优先级排序。

Result: 在七个MDP环境中，该方法有效区分三个开源LLM：推理模型通过85%的测试用例，中型模型通过70%，而1B模型低于随机基线；优先级排序比随机选择能发现显著更难的用例。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM生成解释在无模型设定下的可信度水平，其中没有预言机可用于验证，为评估LLM解释忠实度提供了系统性方法框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used as post hoc explainers of sequential decision-making policies, producing natural-language explanations of why an action was chosen. However, LLMs often generate plausible but incorrect statements, and no existing approach systematically tests whether such explanations are faithful to the underlying environment. Two classic software testing challenges stand in the way: there is no oracle for the correctness of an explanation, and the test inputs, natural language queries about a policy's behavior, lack the structure needed for systematic test case generation. We address both. Probabilistic model checking provides the test oracle, computing exact reference results against which LLM answers are graded automatically. A taxonomy of post hoc query categories structures the input space around the environment-level facts from which policy explanations are composed; test cases generated from it are prioritized by question-specific diagnostic difficulty scores. Across seven MDP environments, the testing separates three open-weight LLMs: a reasoning model passes 85% of test cases, a mid-size model 70%, and a 1B model falls below the random baseline, while prioritization surfaces significantly harder cases than random selection. Our results indicate how trustworthy LLM-generated explanations are in model-free settings, where the same LLMs are used but no oracle exists to verify them.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry of Divergence: Tracking Hidden-State Trajectories for Adaptive Multi-Turn Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30650)
*Jie Liang,Zhengxin Yu,Hamid Nasiri,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用LLM多轮推理过程中隐状态轨迹的几何特征（时间曲率与方差斜率）来识别推理漂移，并在τ-Bench上通过关键转折点干预将任务成功率从24.1%提升至39.6%，同时降低11.2%的token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互中上下文逐步累积会破坏底层LLM对早期任务相关信息的内部表征，导致推理漂移问题，使得"建设性推理"与"表征漂移"之间的边界模糊。现有方法缺乏对推理过程质量的动态监测手段，需要一种在资源受限条件下提前识别推理偏离的信号。

Method: 将多轮推理形式化为底层LLM的隐状态轨迹，通过两个互补几何信号进行刻画：(1) 时间曲率（temporal curvature）——捕捉逐轮更新的方向一致性；(2) 方差斜率（variance slope）——衡量探索空间的扩张或收缩。在四个任务和三个底层LLM上进行实验验证，并将每个回合分解为三个动作链（由Read、Write、Respond、Transfer四种动作构成），分析几何信号在不同动作链模式下的区分能力。

Result: 实验表明这些几何信号能在推理完成前区分正确与错误的episode；且可分离性与动作类型相关，不同信号对不同的动作链模式具有区分力。基于轨迹几何识别关键转折点进行干预，在τ-Bench上将任务成功率从24.1%提升至39.6%，同时token成本降低11.2%。

Conclusion: 隐状态轨迹的几何特征（时间曲率与方差斜率）提供了一种轻量且有效的推理质量监测方法，能够提前识别推理关键转折点并优化推理过程，在提升任务成功率的同时降低计算资源消耗，为长程多轮LLM智能体推理提供了全新的诊断与干预框架。

Abstract: LLM agents need to sustain goal-consistent reasoning across long multi-turn interactions under strict resource constraints. However, as the multi-turn context accumulates, it can destabilize the underlying LLM's internal representation of task-relevant information from earlier turns, blurring the boundary between constructive reasoning and representation drift. We formulate multi-turn reasoning as a hidden-state trajectory of the underlying LLM that is characterized via two complementary signals: temporal curvature that captures the directional consistency of turn-to-turn updates, and variance slope which measures the expansion or contraction of the exploration space. Across four tasks and three underlying LLMs, we observed that these geometric signals distinguish between correct and incorrect episodes prior to completion. We further decompose each episode into three-action chains formed from four actions (Read, Write, Respond, Transfer) and show that separability is action-dependent, with different signals distinguishing various chain patterns. Our experiments demonstrate that trajectory geometry can identify critical turns in the reasoning process, increasing task success rates on $τ$-Bench from 24.1% to 39.6% while reducing token cost by 11.2%.

</details>


### [65] [ATLAS: Dual-Horizon Diagnostic Evaluation for Industrial Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30685)
*Wei Chen,Peilun Zhou,Zhaoyu Hu,Jiajun Chai,Zhongni Hou,Yufei Zhang,Derong Xu,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS，一个面向工业工具使用型LLM智能体的双时间尺度诊断评测框架，涵盖请求轨迹和用户交互两个层面，通过可执行诊断信号提供结构化评估，并在美团小团生产环境中验证了其在离线诊断和在线业务指标上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级智能体服务的评估不能只看最终结果，因为问题可能出现在迭代轨迹的中间环节，也可能在跨轮次的持续交互中积累。传统的最终结果评估难以定位缺陷来源，也无法判断后续服务是否与早期对话上下文保持一致。

Method: ATLAS采用双时间尺度框架：在请求层面（request horizon），通过轨迹级诊断信号将缺陷定位到执行位置和能力类别；在交互层面（interaction horizon），通过用户级信号评估跨轮次的服务响应性。诊断信号具有明确的证据范围和决策边界，底层使用LLM判断器，并针对真实业务日志中的高置信参考进行校准；必要时将判断行为蒸馏为高效诊断模型以降低延迟和成本。

Result: 在美团小团生产流量上完成评估：离线实验验证了诊断信号保真度和基于重放（replay）的策略优化效果；在线A/B实验在用户参与度、下游业务结果和人工审计质量抽样上均获得同步提升。

Conclusion: ATLAS证明同时考虑请求轨迹和用户交互两个层面的诊断性评测，能够为工业工具使用型智能体提供更精细的缺陷定位和持续服务行为分析，其反馈可有效支撑策略优化，并带来可量化的业务收益。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in user-facing services that require iterative tool use under dynamic business conditions. Reliable evaluation is essential for sustained improvement: it must reveal capability deficiencies, inform priorities, and assess interventions. Yet industrial agent service unfolds both through the iterative trajectory of a current request and through continued user interaction. Final-outcome assessment can therefore obscure where deficiencies arise and whether later service remains aligned with context from earlier exchanges. We propose ATLAS, a dual-horizon diagnostic evaluation framework for industrial tool-use agents. At the request horizon, trajectory-wise diagnostic signals relate deficiencies to execution locations and capability concerns. At the interaction horizon, user-wise signals assess whether service remains responsive across continued interaction. Together, these views provide structured diagnostic evidence for analyzing execution deficiencies and sustained service behavior. ATLAS instantiates them as executable signals with explicit evidence scopes and decision boundaries. LLM judge interfaces are calibrated against high-confidence references from real business logs; when needed, their decision behavior is distilled into efficient diagnostic models for lower-latency, lower-cost evaluation. The resulting feedback supports policy optimization. We evaluate ATLAS on Meituan Xiaotuan production traffic. Offline experiments assess diagnostic-signal fidelity and replay-based policy improvement, while online A/B experiments show concurrent gains in user engagement, downstream business outcomes, and sampled human-audit quality.

</details>


### [66] [Predicting Residential Rents in Dakar Using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30865)
*Amadou Tidiane Kassa Diallo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对达喀尔住宅租赁市场数据稀缺的问题，构建了一套从数据采集到模型解释的完整机器学习管线。通过系统网络爬虫构建了1,507条租赁列表的原创数据集，采用贝叶斯优化的XGBoost模型实现R²=0.847的预测性能，并通过SHAP与XGBoost gain的对比揭示了目标编码分类变量的方法论影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达喀尔住宅租赁市场在经济和社会层面具有重要意义（54.4%的家庭为租房者，全国仅23.3%），但该市场的数据记录严重不足，缺乏系统的租金预测研究和基准模型。

Method: 构建了完整ML管线：(1) 通过系统网络爬虫构建1,507条租赁列表数据集，含文档化清洗流程；(2) 设计四个专属特征（含豪华度评分和基于关键词的质量评分）；(3) 比较五种模型（线性回归、Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM，经Optuna贝叶斯优化）；(4) 采用无泄漏的KFold目标编码处理区位变量；(5) 使用XGBoost原生gain和SHAP值进行特征重要性评估。

Result: 优化后的XGBoost模型表现最佳：R²=0.847，MAE=210,902西非法郎，RMSE=324,195西非法郎。特征重要性分析发现位置变量在gain指标中被视为次要预测因子，但在SHAP中却排名第二，二者存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究为达喀尔租赁市场提供了可解释的基准模型，其关于目标编码分类变量在特征重要性评估中的差异发现对享乐价格研究具有方法论意义，并指出未来可整合地理空间特征和保形预测以进一步提升。

Abstract: Dakar's residential rental market remains poorly documented despite its economic and social importance: 54.4% of households are renters, compared to 23.3% nationally. This study develops a complete machine learning pipeline to predict residential rents in Dakar, from data collection to model interpretation. An original dataset of 1,507 rental listings was built through systematic web scraping and a documented cleaning pipeline, then enriched with four purpose-built features, including a luxury score and a keyword-based quality score. Five models were compared: linear regression, Random Forest (baseline), XGBoost, and LightGBM optimized through Bayesian optimization with Optuna, using leakage-free KFold target encoding for location. The optimized XGBoost model achieved the best performance with an $R^2$ of 0.847, an MAE of 210,902 XOF, and an RMSE of 324,195 XOF. Feature importance was assessed using native XGBoost gain and SHAP values, revealing a substantial difference in the ranking of location, which appears as a minor predictor by gain but as the second most influential variable by SHAP. This result carries methodological implications for hedonic studies using target-encoded categorical variables. This study provides an interpretable benchmark for Dakar's rental market and highlights several avenues for improvement, including the integration of geospatial features and conformal prediction.

</details>


### [67] [Wrong Prediction, Right Answer: Recovering Evidence from Collapsed LLM Sequence Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31068)
*Qiyao Yan,Chenpeng Wang,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在推理任务中的失败往往不是缺乏推理能力，而是输出阶段的结构性偏置导致的瓶颈。通过在隐藏状态上计算正确分数而非原生序列评分，并仅用25个无标签样例拟合两个加法校正参数，即可显著恢复9-34个准确率百分点，表明许多零样本推理缺陷实为表达失败而非内在逻辑缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测将模型推理失败直接归因于能力缺失，但忽略了推理生成（内部逻辑）与输出解码（序列生成）两个阶段的区分，导致对模型真实能力的误判。

Method: 提出诊断协议：用隐藏状态探针解码正确答案，对比原生序列评分；引入无需目标标签的加法校正方法，仅拟合两个参数，在25个无标签样例上进行校准，并跨模型（Qwen3.5、OLMo-2-1B、Llama-3.1-8B）验证，同时设置排列置换基线进行对照。

Result: 两个参数在25个无标签样例上即可恢复9-34个准确率点；校正后的决策在困难实例上优于简单词法重叠，显著超过计数保持的排列置换基线，且跨模型迁移成功。

Conclusion: 许多表面上零样本推理的缺陷实际上是表达失败（输出瓶颈）掩盖了完好的内部逻辑，敦促研究界对基准评测结果进行更审慎、更狭窄的解读，不能简单将推理失败等同于能力缺失。

Abstract: When a large language model fails a reasoning task, it is often assumed to lack the underlying capability. However, this conflates a genuine absence of reasoning with a late-stage output bottleneck. We observe a consistent readout gap across diverse reasoning benchmarks: hidden-state probes successfully decode correct answers even when native sequence scoring completely collapses due to structural biases. To test whether instance-specific logic survives this collapse, we introduce a diagnostic protocol using a minimal, target-label-free additive correction. Fitting just two parameters on as few as 25 unlabeled examples recovers 9--34 accuracy points for Qwen3.5 models, transferring successfully to OLMo-2-1B and Llama-3.1-8B. Crucially, these recovered decisions persist on hard instances unresolved by simple lexical overlap and significantly exceed count-preserving permutation baselines. Our results show that many apparent zero-shot reasoning deficits are expression failures masking intact internal logic, urging a narrower interpretation of benchmark evaluations.

</details>


### [68] [Token-Efficient Data Reasoning Agents via Adaptive Structuring of Unstructured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31082)
*Milad Rezaei Hajidehi,Qitong Wang,Stratos Idreos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"代理式数据破解"（Agentic Data Cracking）方法，让LLM代理在回答问题的推理过程中，顺带将非结构化文档进行自适应、推测性的结构化处理。通过从已加载的上下文分叉出子代理提取未来的有用结构，使得越来越多的查询可通过预结构化数据以数据库查询的方式廉价回答，从而在不损失准确率的前提下大幅降低token成本（在FanOutQA上降低53%成本）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业中大量有价值的数据以非结构化形式存在（网页、合同、财报、PDF等），LLM代理需要反复打开大型文档寻找散落证据来回答问题，每次消耗高达百万级token，成本高昂。若数据预先结构化，同样的查询可降级为廉价的数据库查找（在FanOutQA上便宜28倍）。但预先结构化所有内容不可行，因为文档中蕴含的潜在结构远超任何工作负载所需，且有用的结构和文档在查询到来前是未知的。因此需要一种自适应、按需的结构化方法。

Method: 作者提出"代理式数据破解"：当代理打开一份文档进行推理时，一个"破解子代理"（cracking sub-agent）从已加载的上下文中分叉出来，以边际成本从文档中提取"有根据的"（grounded）结构，并寻找可能服务未来相关查询的内容。结构化是自适应的（由观察到的查询决定何时发生及何者重要）和推测性的（超越当前问题进行提取）。随着时间推移，越来越多的查询完全由已结构化数据覆盖，无需再打开原始文档即可回答，从而将代理式准确率保持在接近RAG的成本水平。

Result: 在FanOutQA基准上，仅扩展每个测试问题附带一个相关问题，该方法即可削减53%的成本同时保持准确率。此外，在理想预结构化存储上推理比原始方式便宜28倍，且随着问题在更多文档上扇出，成本差距扩大到数量级。

Conclusion: 代理式数据破解是迈向下一代面向非结构化数据的代理推理数据基础设施的第一步——在模型下方构建一个共享基板，让推理过程已经付费发现的知识得以积累复用。它展示了将推理副产品转化为持久结构化资产的可能性，为未来企业级代理系统提供了成本可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Valuable data remains embedded in unstructured sources: web pages, reports, contracts, filings, earnings calls, and PDFs. The big bet in enterprise AI is deploying LLM agents that reason over this data to answer complex questions for every knowledge worker. Agents can do this today, but at prohibitive cost. Each question repeatedly opens large documents to recover scattered evidence, consuming up to a million tokens. However, if the data were already structured, the same question would reduce to a cheap database lookup. For example, on FanOutQA benchmark, reasoning over an ideal pre-structured store is 28X cheaper, and the gap grows to orders of magnitude as questions fan out over more documents. Yet structuring everything in advance is not viable: documents hold vastly more possible structure than any workload will use, and the useful structure and documents are unknown until queries arrive. We propose agentic data cracking, a method that structures unstructured data adaptively and speculatively as a byproduct of reasoning itself. Structuring is adaptive because observed queries decide when it happens and what matters, and speculative because it goes beyond the current question. Whenever the agent opens a document to answer, a cracking sub-agent forks from the already-loaded context at marginal cost and extracts grounded structure likely to serve related future queries. Over time, an increasing share of queries is fully covered by structured data and answered without opening a document, keeping agentic accuracy at close to RAG cost. On FanOutQA, extended with merely one related question per test question, cracking cuts cost by 53% while preserving accuracy. Agentic data cracking is a first step toward next-generation data infrastructure for agentic reasoning over unstructured data: a shared substrate beneath the model where knowledge that reasoning already paid to uncover accumulates.

</details>


### [69] [BLOOM-WILT: Logit Tilting for Behaviour Elicitation in Automated LLM Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31105)
*Adrians Skapars,Edoardo Manino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BLOOM-WILT是一个完整的审计流水线，通过自适应对话策略调整和基于模型自身分布的解码重加权，无需训练成本即可高效诱发部署语言模型中的罕见行为，在4个目标模型和8种行为上显著超越基线审计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的语言模型会经历远超任何评估所能模拟的交互量，导致罕见行为在测试中几乎无法被发现。现有自动化审计器虽然成本低、覆盖灵活，但由于缺乏优化压力而采样效率低下，难以在有限预算内有效诱发罕见行为。

Method: BLOOM-WILT采用双端优化：(1) 输入端，审计器模型跨轮次修正对话策略，从先前打分交互中学习；(2) 输出端，利用目标模型自身在诱发提示条件下的分布自适应重加权解码，使行为相关生成优先于其他同等概率的生成，且无需训练成本或超出目标模型next-token分布之外的访问权限。

Result: 在4个目标模型和8种行为上评估，WILT在32个设置中的30个击败基线审计器，并推翻了此前的模型安全排名。在从Qwen3.5-4B引出自我伤害鼓励行为时，平均行为出现率从51%提升到100%，在同等计算资源下击败所有移植到同一流水线的诱发方法，且未将输出概率压低至基线以下。

Conclusion: BLOOM-WILT通过同时优化审计对话策略和解码偏向，实现了高效、低成本、高覆盖率的自动化行为审计，能够更准确地揭示部署模型中的真实风险并修正评估排名偏差。

Abstract: Users of a deployed language model routinely encounter behaviours that testing almost never surfaces, since deployment puts the model through orders of magnitude more interactions than any evaluation can simulate. Automated auditors make testing cheap to scale and flexible enough to cover almost any specified behaviour, yet their lack of optimisation pressure makes them sample-inefficient. To address this shortcoming, we introduce BLOOM-WILT, a full auditing pipeline that elicits natural multi-turn instances of rare behaviours, without training cost or access beyond the target's next-token distribution. On the input side, WILT's auditor model revises its conversational strategy across rounds, learning from previous scored interactions. On the output side, WILT adaptively reweights the target's decoding using the model's own distribution conditioned on an elicitation prompt, so that behaviour-relevant generations are sampled ahead of others it finds equally probable when unprompted. We evaluate WILT across 4 target models and 8 behaviours, where it beats the baseline auditor in 30 of the 32 settings and overturns the previous model safety rankings. WILT raises average behaviour presence from 51% to 100% when eliciting self-harm encouragement from Qwen3.5-4B, beating every elicitation method we port into the same pipeline at matched compute, without pushing output probability below the baseline's.

</details>


### [70] [When Does Bigger Help? A Controlled Study of LLM Scale for Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31118)
*Hamed Babaei Giglou,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对13种大语言模型（涵盖稠密和混合专家变体，来自Qwen3.5/Qwen3.6系列以及GPT发布版本）在生物医学和材料科学与工程四个本体上的本体学习性能进行了受控对比评估。研究发现模型规模对本体学习的影响既不单调也不均匀：在稠密模型中参数量增加主要提升精确率而非召回率，9B到27B之间的增益最大；稠密27B模型在术语类型标注上优于更大的稀疏模型，而更大的MoE模型在分类体系发现上表现最佳；非分类关系提取在所有规模下均具挑战性。模型架构和血统可能比名义参数量更重要，模型大小不足以单独作为本体学习的选择标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型规模对本体学习性能的影响尚未得到充分刻画，现有研究缺乏在相同嵌入模型、检索配置、提示模板、解码设置、数据集和指标下的系统对照评估，这导致模型选择缺乏实证指导。

Method: 采用OntoLearner检索增强生成流水线，对13个模型进行受控评估，涵盖Qwen3.5和Qwen3.6系列的稠密与MoE变体及GPT发布版本。所有模型在相同配置下评估其在术语类型标注、分类体系发现和非分类关系提取三个任务上的表现，涉及四个生物医学和材料科学与工程本体。

Result: 稠密Qwen3.5系列中，参数量增加主要提升精确率而非召回率，最大增益出现在9B到27B之间。规模效应并非单调均匀：稠密27B模型在术语类型标注上优于更大的稀疏模型，而更大MoE模型在分类体系发现上获得最佳开源结果。非分类关系提取在所有规模下均困难，尤其是材料数据科学本体。匹配的Qwen变体与GPT版本间的性能差异表明架构和血统可超越名义参数量。

Conclusion: 模型大小单独不足以作为本体学习中的模型选择标准。研究为可复现的LLM辅助本体工程提供了经验性指导，表明需同时考虑模型架构、血统和具体任务需求。

Abstract: The effect of Large Language Model (LLM) scale on ontology learning (OL) performance remains insufficiently characterized. We present a controlled evaluation of 13 models spanning dense and Mixture-of-Experts variants from the Qwen3.5 and Qwen3.6 lineages, together with proprietary GPT release variants, using the OntoLearner retrieval-augmented generation pipeline. All models are evaluated with the same embedding model, retrieval configuration, prompt templates, decoding settings, datasets, and metrics on term typing, taxonomy discovery, and non-taxonomic relationship extraction across four biomedical and materials science and engineering ontologies. Within the dense Qwen3.5 lineage, increasing parameter count primarily improves precision rather than recall, with the largest gains occurring between 9B and 27B parameters. However, the effect of scale is neither monotonic nor uniform across tasks and domains. Dense 27B models outperform substantially larger sparse models on term typing, whereas larger Mixture-of-Experts models achieve the strongest open-weight results on taxonomy discovery. Non-taxonomic relationship extraction remains difficult across model scales, particularly for the Materials Data Science ontology. Performance differences across matched Qwen variants and proprietary GPT releases further indicate that architecture and model lineage can outweigh nominal parameter count. These findings show that model size alone is an insufficient selection criterion for OL and provide empirical guidance for reproducible LLM-assisted ontology engineering.

</details>


### [71] [OntoAligner-Ensemble: Voting-Based Fusion across Heterogeneous Ontology Alignment Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31137)
*Hamed Babaei Giglou,Sören Auer,Peio Popov,Mahsa Sanaei,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OntoAligner-Ensemble，一个模块化且对齐器无关的框架，通过两阶段过程（基于投票的融合策略+融合后选择策略）系统性地整合不同的本体对齐器（从字符串对齐器到KGE模型再到LLM方法）的候选对应关系。在来自五个OAEI基准轨道上的八个任务中，集成融合持续改善了精确率与召回率的平衡，且交叉范式的异构集成通常提升精确率，而同质LLM集成更常获得更高的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体对齐领域从词法和结构对齐器进化到知识图谱嵌入模型和LLM方法，虽然现代OA框架提供了统一生态系统来部署这些异构对齐器，但系统性地调和它们互补甚至冲突的预测结果的机制仍然相对未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一个模块化、对齐器无关的两阶段集成框架：第一阶段采用基于投票的融合策略合并各对齐器的候选对应关系；第二阶段应用融合后选择策略来筛选最终结果。该框架支持OntoAligner中任何能产生候选对应关系的对齐器，并通过代表性轻量字符串对齐器、KGE模型、基于RAG的LLM对齐器（包括开源权重和API基LLM）进行实例化。

Result: 在来自五个OAEI轨道的八个基准任务（涵盖生物医学到超越等价关系）上的评估表明：集成融合一致地改善了精确率与召回率之间的平衡，并在多样化领域中频繁超越独立对齐器；异构交叉范式集成通常提升精确率，而同质LLM集成更常获得更高的整体F1分数。

Conclusion: 系统性集成学习为OA提供了一种稳健且可复现的策略，同时为不同对齐场景下选择集成构成提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Ontology alignment (OA) has evolved through several methodological paradigms, ranging from lexical and structural aligners to knowledge graph embedding (KGE) models and, more recently, Large Language Model (LLM)-based approaches. Although modern OA frameworks provide unified ecosystems for deploying these heterogeneous aligners, mechanisms for systematically reconciling their complementary and sometimes conflicting predictions remain relatively underexplored. We present OntoAligner-Ensemble, a modular and aligner-agnostic framework that combines candidate correspondences through a configurable two-stage process comprising voting-based fusion strategies followed by post-fusion selection policies. The framework supports any aligner implemented within OntoAligner that produces candidate correspondences, enabling diverse alignment paradigms to be integrated through a unified decision process. To demonstrate its effectiveness, we instantiate the framework using representative lightweight string-aligner, KGE-based, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation aligners powered by both open-weight and API-based LLMs. We evaluate individual aligners and ensemble configurations across eight benchmark tasks from five OAEI tracks spanning biomedical to beyond-equivalence. The results show that ensemble fusion consistently improves the balance between precision and recall and frequently outperforms standalone aligners across diverse domains. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that ensemble composition directly affects the precision-recall trade-off: heterogeneous cross-paradigm ensembles generally improve precision, whereas homogeneous LLM ensembles more often achieve higher overall F1-scores. These findings demonstrate that systematic ensemble learning offers a robust and reproducible strategy for OA while providing practical guidance for selecting ensemble compositions under different alignment scenarios.

</details>

### [72] [Cesium Clustering and Fluoroberyllate Network Disruption in FLiBe: A Total Scattering and Molecular Dynamics Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29898)
*Sean Fayfar,Rajni Chahal,Danny Wang,David J. Sprouster,Shravan Venugopal,Guiqiu Zheng,Dan Olds,Joerg Neuefeind,Stephen Lam,Boris Khaykovich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过X射线和中子衍射实验、经验势结构精修（EPSR）建模及神经网络分子动力学（NNMD）模拟，系统研究了含5 mol% CsF的FLiBe熔盐中铯对局域及中程结构的影响。结果显示Cs轻微破坏氟铍酸盐网络、增加自由氟离子比例，Cs离子发生广泛团簇且由BeF4^2-四面体桥接，同时5 mol% CsF抑制了室温下Li2BeF4晶相的形成（仅在加热至180°C以上出现）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代裂变反应堆设计采用FLiBe等熔盐作为冷却剂甚至直接溶解燃料，运行中裂变产物（如铯）会在盐中积累，可能改变熔盐的热物理性质及原子结构。理解含裂变产物后熔盐的结构扰动，对准确预测反应堆运行行为至关重要。

Method: 采用实验与模拟相结合的方法：1）对FLiBe+5 mol% CsF进行X射线和中子衍射测量；2）利用经验势结构精修（EPSR）对实验数据进行建模；3）使用神经网络分子动力学（NNMD）模拟进行结构对比，以区分散射数据约束的结构特征与模型依赖的结构特征。

Result: Cs相关的关联解释了总结构因子和配对分布函数的变化，而FLiBe本身的关联几乎不变。Cs轻微扰乱中程氟铍酸盐网络，增加自由氟离子比例，但局域配位基本不变。Cs离子在FLiBe中广泛团簇，BeF4^2-四面体起桥接相邻Cs环境的作用。此外，添加5 mol% CsF抑制了室温Li2BeF4晶相的形成，该相仅在加热至180°C以上才出现。

Conclusion: 实验约束下的结构特征为预测含裂变产物FLiBe行为的原子模型提供了基准。研究表明Cs对熔盐液态结构的扰动较小，但对结晶行为有显著影响（抑制Li2BeF4低温相形成），这对反应堆中熔盐的长期运行与材料设计具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Several next-generation fission reactor designs employ molten salts such as FLiBe (2LiF-BeF$_2$), with some concepts using fuel dissolved directly in the salt. During operation, fission products such as cesium will accumulate in the salt mixture, potentially leading to an evolution of the thermophysical properties underpinned by the atomic structure. To understand the structural perturbations in FLiBe with 5 mol% CsF, we conducted X-ray and neutron diffraction measurements, refined empirical potential structure refinement (EPSR) models against the experimental data, and compared the resulting structure with neural network molecular dynamics (NNMD) simulations. Comparisons of the EPSR and NNMD structures distinguishes features constrained by the scattering data from those that remain model dependent. The new Cs-bearing correlations account for the changes in the total structure factor and pair-distribution function, while the FLiBe correlations remain minimally altered. We find that Cs slightly disrupts the intermediate-range fluoroberyllate network, increasing the fraction of free fluorine ions, while the local coordination remains largely unchanged. The Cs ions within FLiBe cluster extensively, with BeF$_4^{2-}$ tetrahedra bridging neighboring cesium environments. In contrast to the minor structural perturbations in the liquid, the addition of 5 mol% CsF suppressed the formation of the crystalline Li2BeF4 phase at room temperature, with the phase appearing only above 180C upon heating. These experimentally constrained structural features provide a benchmark for atomistic models used to predict the behavior and properties of fission-product-containing FLiBe.

</details>


### [73] [Revealing low-energy surfaces of multinary compounds by controlling surface coordination environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28903)
*Weihang Xie,Harshan Reddy Gopidi,Zhengyu Liu,Romain Claes,Alexander G. Squires,Keith T. Butler,David O. Scanlon,Pieremanuele Canepa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文介绍SALAMI，一个Python包，用于生成多组分化合物的对称、电荷中性、无偶极且低能量的表面模型。通过对Li3PS4和ZnSb2O6所有对称不等价晶向的系统研究，发现保持最优配位环境能显著降低表面能，使Wulff形状体积缩小约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分化合物表面建模中，传统解理面往往会切断强键合的多面体单元，导致产生物理上不合理的表面能。亟需一种系统的方法来生成保留最佳配位环境的低能量表面模型。

Method: 作者开发了SALAMI软件包，通过组合搜索选择性去除表面原子，生成具有波纹状（corrugated）的终止面，从而保持最优配位环境。该方法应用于两种典型结构（固态电解质Li3PS4和透明导电氧化物ZnSb2O6）的所有对称不等价晶向（Miller指数至2），并结合DFT计算验证表面能。

Result: DFT计算表明：Li3PS4必须保留所有PS4四面体单元才能获得最低表面能；ZnSb2O6的低能表面则需要表面Sb原子从体相SbO6部分欠配位至SbO5或SbO4。施加配位约束生成的表面模型相比无约束模型，能显著降低表面能，使预测的Wulff形状体积缩小约20%。

Conclusion: 对多组分化合物表面能进行准确预测，必须精细控制局域配位环境。SALAMI方法为多组分化合物表面的第一性原理计算提供了可靠的低能量表面模型生成工具。

Abstract: When modeling surfaces of multinary compounds, conventional cleavage planes often cut through strongly bonded polyhedra, resulting in unphysical surface energies. Here, we introduce SALAMI (Symmetric Atomic Layers for Arbitrary Multinary Interfaces), a Python package that generates symmetric, charge-neutral, dipole-free, and low-energy slab models for multinary compounds. SALAMI performs combinatorial searches to selectively remove surface atoms and generate corrugated terminations that preserve optimal coordination environments. We applied this workflow to all symmetrically inequivalent crystallographic orientations with Miller indices up to 2 for two prototypical structures: the solid-state electrolyte Li3PS4 and the transparent conducting oxide ZnSb2O6. Density functional theory calculations reveal that Li3PS4 must preserve all PS4 units to achieve the minimum surface energy. For ZnSb2O6, low-energy surfaces are achieved by partial undercoordination of surface Sb atoms to SbO5 or SbO4 from the bulk SbO6, depending on the surface orientations. Compared to surface models generated with unconstrained coordination, applying constraints to achieve optimal local coordination environments significantly lowers surface energies, shrinking the volume of the predicted Wulff shape by approximately 20%. Our results demonstrate that meticulous control of local coordination environments is necessary for accurately predicting the surface energetics of multinary compounds.

</details>


### [74] [Chemically Resolved Topological Coordinates Link Structural Dynamics and Configurational Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30523)
*Ayush Kumar Pandey,Abhishek Tewari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出化学导向持久同调（chemically directed persistent homology）方法，在四个分辨率（完整网络、位点、空间场、替代排列）下保留化学身份和长度尺度来分析原子运动与构型能量排序。应用于CsPbI3的AIMD轨迹和替代掺杂体系，发现γ相局部软性与网络记忆丧失是独立的，特定Pb位点的拓扑异常可预测后续位移，并揭示许多DFT能量误差小的学习模型会反转DFT的构型能序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子坐标只能规定结构，无法直接揭示跨多个长度尺度的化学连接性如何与原子运动和构型能量排序相关联。需要一个保留化学身份与长度尺度的拓扑工具，将结构网络与动力学行为及能量排序联系起来，并检验机器学习势函数是否真正保留DFT的构型有序性。

Method: 提出四个分辨率的化学导向持久同调框架：(1) 完整网络、(2) 单个位点、(3) 空间场、(4) 替代排列。对δ-和γ-CsPbI3在500-700K五个温度的AIMD轨迹进行分析，提取Pb-I恢复刚度、碘化物偏移、位置相关衰减、Pb网络拓扑记忆等动力学指标；并利用同一Pb网络坐标分析δ|γ相界（2560原子）和替代掺杂体系的构型能量排序。

Result: γ相（角共享）Pb-I恢复刚度更低，允许更大的碘偏移，但碘位置相关衰减比δ相慢2.27倍，Pb网络拓扑记忆长1.63倍。在替代体系中，紧凑掺杂排列具有更大的协同宿主弛豫且DFT能量更低。SchNet和Allegro模型虽然平均能量误差相当，但在该坐标上的排序相反；247个误差低于1 meV/atom的模型中，有61个反转了DFT关系。

Conclusion: 局部软性与网络记忆丧失在钙钛矿中是两个独立的概念。化学网络拓扑将结构连接到物理响应，并能有效测试学习型能量模型是否保留DFT的构型有序性——即使平均误差很小也可能在关键拓扑坐标上反转能量排序，这直接影响玻尔兹曼群体的准确性。

Abstract: Atomic coordinates specify a structure, but they do not reveal how chemical connectivity across several length scales relates to atomic motion and configurational energy ordering. We formulate chemically directed persistent homology at four resolutions---complete networks, individual sites, spatial fields, and substitutional arrangements---while retaining the chemical identity and length scale of each connectivity feature. In \textit{ab initio} molecular dynamics (AIMD) trajectories of $δ$- and $γ$-CsPbI$_3$ at five temperatures spanning 500--700 K, the corner-sharing $γ$ network has a lower Pb--I restoring stiffness and permits larger iodide excursions, yet iodide positional correlations decay 2.27 times more slowly and Pb-network topology retains memory 1.63 times longer than in the edge-sharing $δ$ phase. Local softness and loss of network memory are therefore distinct. At individual sites, rare $γ$-phase Pb environments with a $δ$-like Cs-cage connectivity precede 0.17 $\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$ greater Pb displacement over the subsequent 0.5 ps. The same Pb-network coordinate resolves disruption of corner-sharing connectivity across a 2560-atom $δ|γ$ boundary. In substituted CsPbI$_3$, compact dopant arrangements undergo greater cooperative host relaxation and lie lower in density-functional-theory (DFT) energy than dispersed arrangements of the same composition. SchNet and Allegro model families with comparable energy errors encode opposite ordering along this coordinate, and 61 of 247 supplied models with errors below 1 meV atom$^{-1}$ on separate test structures reverse the DFT relation. Because relative configurational energies set Boltzmann populations, chemical network topology links structure to physical response and tests whether learned energy models preserve DFT configurational ordering even when their average errors are small.

</details>


### [75] [Symmetry-Preserving Phase Transitions in $AM_2$Al$_9$ Materials under Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28957)
*Jian-Feng Zhang,Sheng Xu,Zhong-Yi Lu,Tao Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算揭示了AM₂Al₉化合物（A = Ba, Ca, Sr, Eu；M = Fe, Co, Ni）在压力驱动下发生同构相变，该相变保留了P6/mmm空间群对称性但伴随显著的体积坍缩，其微观机制由层内与层间原子键的键合特征再分配所驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同构结构相变（保留母相对称性的一级相变）在材料中极为罕见，但其在理解临界现象和对称性保持的结构转变方面具有重要科学价值。传统的结构相变通常伴随对称性改变，而同构相变作为一种特殊例外，缺乏合适的单晶材料平台进行实验研究，因此探索和确认此类相变体系具有重要的理论和实验意义。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，对一系列AM₂Al₉化合物（A = Ba, Ca, Sr, Eu；M = Fe, Co, Ni）进行压力诱导的结构相变模拟。利用晶体轨道Hamilton布居（ICOHP）的积分分析手段来定量评估原子间键合强度的变化，从而揭示相变背后的键合特征再分配机制。

Abstract: Orbital-lattice coupling in transition-metal oxides is usually discussed within a given bonding framework, where orbital occupation is intertwined with local coordination, strain, or symmetry breaking. Abstract: Photocatalytic water splitting offers a viable pathway for sustainable hydrogen production. The heterostructure shows type-II band alignment with an indirect band gap of 2.17 eV, smaller than those of the individual g-C3N4 (2.81 eV) and GeS (3.31 eV) monolayers. AIMD simulations and optical absorption in the visible region confirm the thermodynamic stability and promising photocatalytic potential of the heterostructure for hydrogen generation.

Abstract: Amorphous solid-state electrolytes are attractive candidates for safe, high-energy-density all-solid-state batteries, yet their mechanical properties remain poorly understood from a computational perspective. Abstract: Despite the barocaloric performance recently reported for the spin-crossover complex Fe(pap-5NO2)2, the microscopic origin of its pressure-induced entropy change remains poorly understood.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Zinc batteries offer a compelling route to safe and low-cost energy storage, yet their reliance on aqueous electrolytes promotes hydrogen evolution, corrosion, cathode dissolution, and non-uniform zinc deposition. Replacing the liquid with a solid or quasi-solid electrolyte can suppress these processes, but it also removes the medium that enables rapid Zn2+ transport and conformal electrode contact. This tension, the price of removing water, has been obscured by inconsistent use of the term solid state and by comparisons based largely on bulk ionic conductivity. Here we critically examine zinc electrolytes across a continuum from water-rich hydrogels to dry polymers, solvated crystals, and inorganic conductors. We distinguish water content from thermodynamic water activity and classify these materials according to phase state, mobile-solvent fraction, and dominant transport mechanism. We show that neither high conductivity nor nominally water-free composition reliably predicts cell performance: electrolyte thickness, Zn2+ transference, interfacial resistance, and evolving contact often determine the practical outcome. Controlled-solvation and hybrid electrolytes therefore provide the most credible near-term path, whereas genuinely solvent-free Zn2+ conductors remain a longer-term scientific target. Progress will require transparent reporting of solvent state and validation using thin electrolytes, realistic electrode loadings, limited zinc excess, and calendar-life testing.

</details>
